Correia]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Oliveira]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Teixeira vs. Saint Preux''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 73''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on August 8, 2015, at [[Bridgestone Arena]] in [[Nashville]], [[Tennessee]], United States.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Homecoming-for-OSP-against-Teixeira-in-August|title=Homecoming for OSP against Teixeira in August|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-05-20|access-date=2015-05-20}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a light heavyweight bout between former title challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] and Tennessee resident [[Ovince Saint Preux]].\n\n[[Matt Van Buren]] was briefly linked to a bout with promotional newcomer [[Jonathan Wilson (fighter)|Jonathan Wilson]] at the event. However, Van Buren was removed from the card in early June citing injury and was replaced by [[Jared Cannonier]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/jared-cannonier-in-for-van-buren-vs-jonathan-wilson-at-ufc-fight-night-74-in-nashville|title=Jared Cannonier in for Van Buren vs. Jonathan Wilson at UFC Fight Night 74 in Nashville|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-06-04|access-date=2015-06-04}} In turn, Cannonier was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by [[Chris Dempsey]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Dempsey-Moves-to-205-for-Wilson-Clash|title=Dempsey Moves to 205 for Wilson Clash|publisher=UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-07-22|access-date=2015-07-22}}\n\n[[Joe Riggs]] was expected to face [[Uriah Hall]] at this event. However, Riggs was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Oluwale Bamgbose]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Nashville-Fight-Updates-072315|title=Nashville Fight Updates|publisher=UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-07-23|access-date=2015-07-23}}\n\n[[Ian McCall (fighter)|Ian McCall]] was expected to face [[Dustin Ortiz]] at this event. However, McCall was forced to pull out of the event due to injury and was replaced by [[Willie Gates]]. Gates had previously fought only 27 days before the event, when he defeated [[Darrell Montague]] at [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians#The Ultimate Fighter 21 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter 21 Finale]].\n\n[[Ray Borg]] missed weight on his first attempt at the weigh-ins, coming in at 126.75 lb. After having made no attempts to cut further, he was fined 20 percent of his fight purse, which went to his opponent [[Geane Herrera]].{{cite web|url=http://themmareport.com/2015/08/ufc-fight-night-73-weigh-in-results/|title=UFC Fight Night 73 Weigh-In Holm]]\n}}\n{{italics title}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Belfort vs. Henderson 3''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 77''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on November 7, 2015, at the [[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]] in [[São Paulo|São Paulo, Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/8/2/9086009/vitor-belfort-vs-dan-henderson-headlines-ufc-fight-night-card-in|title=Vitor Belfort vs. Dan Henderson headlines UFC Fight Night card in November|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-08-02|access-date=2015-08-02}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Belfort-Hendo-III-Headlines-in-Sao-Paulo|title=Belfort-Hendo III headlines in São Paulo|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-08-02|access-date=2015-08-02}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the fourth held in [[São Paulo]] following [[UFC Brazil|UFC Ultimate Brazil]] (1998), [[UFC on FX: Belfort vs. Bisping]] (2013) and [[The Ultimate Fighter Brazil 3 Finale: Miocic vs. Maldonado]] (2014) and the twenty-fourth overall event to be held in [[Brazil]].\n\nThe event was headlined by a middleweight trilogy bout between longtime multi-divisional contenders and champions [[Vitor Belfort]] and [[Dan Henderson]]. Henderson defeated Belfort in their first encounter in 2006 at [[Pride 32]] via unanimous decision. Belfort defeated Henderson in their second encounter in 2013 at [[UFC Fight Night: Belfort vs. Henderson 2]] via first round knockout.\n\n[[Tom Lawlor]] was expected to face [[Fábio Maldonado]] at this event. However, Lawlor was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn|The Ultimate Fighter 19]] light heavyweight winner [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Anderson-In-for-Injured-Lawlor-in-Sao-Paulo|title=Anderson in for injured Lawlor in São Paulo|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2015-10-20|date=2015-10-20}}\n\nOn December 4, it was announced that [[Gleison Tibau]] was \"provisionally suspended\" by the [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) due to a potential failed out-of-competition drug test.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/4/9851086/gleison-tibau-provisionally-suspended-after-potential-usada-violation|title=Gleison Tibau 'provisionally suspended' after potential USADA violation|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|access-date=2015-12-04|date=2015-12-06}} On December 23, a second potential failed drug test, this time in-competition, was also announced.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/2015/12/gleison-tibau-notified-of-in-competition-testing-failure-his-second-failed-drug-test|title=Gleison Tibau notified of in-competition testing failure, his second failed drug test|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2015-12-23|date=2015-12-23}} On February 16, Tibau \nannounced he will drop his appeal and face the minimum punishment under the new rules, which is a two-year suspension.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/ufc-lightweight-gleison-tibau-says-hell-drop-usada-appeal-accept-two-year-punishment|title=UFC lightweight Gleison Tibau says he'll drop USADA appeal, accept 2-year punishment|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and Christian Stein|access-date=2016-02-16|date=2016-02-16}} A day later, Tibau admitted he used [[erythropoietin]] out-of-competition thinking it \"wouldn’t be anything\" and that he thought he wasn't doing something wrong.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/17/11027858/gleison-tibau-admits-epo-use-i-took-it-without-imagining-i-was-doing|title=Gleison Tibau admits EPO use: 'I took it without imagining I was doing something wrong'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|access-date=2016-02-17|date=2016-02-17}} Subsequently, on February 18, USADA announced Tibau's two-year suspension retroactive to the date of the fight and his win is now overturned to a loss, per the anti-doping policy of the Comissão Atlética Brasileira de MMA (CABMMA), which regulated the bout. Thus, [[Abel Trujillo]]'s loss was overturned to a win.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/18/11054324/usada-officially-suspends-gleison-tibau-for-two-years|title=USADA officially suspends Gleison Tibau for two years|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|access-date=2016-02-18|date=2016-02-18}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports vs. Saint Preux]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 191|UFC 191: Johnson vs. Dodson 2]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Oliveira''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 74''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on August 23, 2015, at the [[SaskTel Centre]] in [[Saskatoon]], [[Saskatchewan]], [[Canada]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Debuts-in-Saskatoon-This-August|title=UFC debuts in Saskatoon this August|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-06-15|access-date=2015-06-15}}{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Dynamic-Featherweight-Contenders-Headline-UFC-Saskatoon-Debut-This-August|title=Dynamic featherweight contenders headline UFC Saskatoon this August|publisher=ufc.com|author=UFC Press Release|date=2015-06-24|access-date=2015-06-24}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/max-holloway-vs-charles-oliveira-headlines-ufcs-debut-in-saskatoon-on-aug-23|title=Max Holloway vs. Charles Oliveira headlines UFC's debut in Saskatoon on Aug. 23|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-06-15|access-date=2015-06-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the organization has held in [[Saskatchewan]] and was headlined by a featherweight bout between rising stars [[Max Holloway]] and [[Charles Oliveira]].\n\nTwo fights, [[Rick Story]] vs. [[Erick Silva]] and [[Marcos Rogério de Lima]] vs. [[Nikita Krylov]] were originally expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Romero]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/5/15/8610043/lyoto-machida-vs-yoel-romero-set-as-new-main-event-for-tuf-brazil-4|title=Lyoto Machida vs. Yoel Romero set as new main event for TUF Brazil: 4 Finale in Miami|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-05-15|access-date=2015-06-24}} However, on June 19, it was announced that several bouts would be postponed and moved to other events as international fighters experienced travel restrictions due to technical issues within the [[Bureau of Consular Affairs]] division of the [[U.S. State Department]], which produces [[visa (document)|travel visas]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Fight-Night-Loses-SilvaStory-TUF-Brazil-4-Finals-from-June-27-Card-in-Florida-87917|title=UFC Fight Night loses Silva-Story, 'TUF: Brazil 4' Finals from June 27 Card in Florida|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2015-06-19|access-date=2015-06-24}} The two pairings were left intact and were rescheduled for this event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/6/24/8839335/rick-story-vs-erick-silva-to-serve-as-ufc-saskatoon-co-main-event|title=Rick Story vs. Erick Silva to serve as UFC Saskatoon co-main event; other fights added to card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2015-06-24|access-date=2015-06-24}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/07/nikita-krylov-vs-marcos-rogerio-de-lima-re-booked-for-ufc-fight-night-74|title=Nikita Krylov vs. Marcos Rogerio de Lima re-booked for UFC Fight Night 74|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-07-03|access-date=2015-07-03}} On August 11, an injury forced Story out of the bout and [[Neil Magny]] stepped in to take his place. Magny had previously fought only 22 days before the event, when he was defeated by [[Demian Maia]] at [[UFC 190]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/neil-magny-in-for-injured-rick-story-faces-erick-silva-at-ufc-fight-night-74|title=Neil Magny in for injured Rick Story, faces Erick Silva at UFC Fight Night 74|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-08-11|access-date=2015-08-11}}\n\n[[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]] was expected to face [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] at the event. However, Wade pulled out of the bout in late July after sustaining an injury and was replaced by [[Tony Sims]].{{cite web|url=http://themmareport.com/2015/07/chris-wade-out-of-ufc-fight-night-74-tony-sims-steps-up-to-meet-olivier-aubin-mercier/|title=Chris Wade out of UFC Fight Night 74, Tony Sims steps up to meet Olivier Aubin-Mercier|publisher=themmareport.com|author=Staff|date=2015-07-30|access-date=2015-07-30}}\n\n[[Sean O'Connell (fighter)|Sean O'Connell]] was expected to face UFC newcomer [[Misha Cirkunov]] at the event. However, O'Connell pulled out of the bout on August 11 due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by another UFC newcomer in Daniel Jolly.{{cite web|url=http://www.mikethetruth.com/2015/08/daniel-jolly-makes-ufc-debut-against-misha-cirkunov-at-fight-night-74/|title=Daniel Jolly makes UFC debut against Misha Cirkunov at Fight Night 74|publisher=mikethetruth.com|author=Mike Jackson|date=2015-08-11|access-date=2015-08-11}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Max Holloway]]\n|def.\n|[[Charles Oliveira]]\n|TKO (esophagus injury)\n|1\n|1:39\n|Oliveira appeared to have injured his neck/shoulder during the fight. Despite initial reports of follow-up exams revealing he suffered an [[esophagus]] micro-tear,{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/ufc-esophagus-tear-forced-charles-oliveiras-early-ufc-fight-night-74-exit|title=UFC: Esophagus tear forced Charles Oliveira's early UFC Fight Night 74 exit|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-08-24|access-date=2015-08-24}} the UFC later released a statement clarifying that Oliveira had no major injuries.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/charles-oliveira-released-from-hospital-ufc-announces|title=Charles Oliveira released from hospital, UFC announces|publisher=mmajunkie.com.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2015-08-25|access-date=2015-08-25}} Oliveira confirmed in an interview that he suffered a minor neck injury, related to a previous injury from his training camp. It will not require surgery.{{cite web|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2015/08/do-bronx-explica-lesao-no-pescoco-e-diz-que-nao-vai-precisar-de-cirurgia.html|title=Do Bronx explica in [[Saitama, Saitama|Saitama]], [[Japan]].{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-josh-barnett-vs-roy-nelson-headlines-ufc-s-return-to-japan-051415|title=Josh Barnett vs. Roy Nelson headlines UFC's return to Japan|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2015-05-14|access-date=2015-05-14}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the fourth that the organization has hosted in [[Saitama City|Saitama]], following [[UFC 144]] in February 2012, [[UFC on Fuel TV: Silva vs. Stann]] in March 2013, and [[UFC Fight Night: Hunt vs. Nelson]] in September 2014.\n\nThe event was headlined by a heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight champion]] [[Josh Barnett]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavyweights|The Ultimate Fighter 10]] heavyweight winner [[Roy Nelson (fighter)|Roy Nelson]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/lineup-set-for-ufc-fight-night-75-nelson-vs-barnett-in-japan|title=Lineup set for 'UFC Fight Night 75: Nelson vs. Barnett' in Japan|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-08-26|access-date=2015-08-26}} Both men had coached opposite each other on the show ''Road to UFC: Japan''.\n\nThe featherweight final of ''Road to UFC: Japan'', a new UFC reality series that was held in conjunction with the event, was featured on the card.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmanews.com/nelson-barnett-set-to-coach-road-to-the-ufc-japan-will-fight-each-other-on-926/|title=Nelson-Barnett set to coach \"Road To The UFC: Japan\"|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Matt Boone|date=2015-05-14|access-date=2015-05-14}}{{cite web|url=http://fightland.vice.com/blog/meet-the-eight-featherweights-who-will-compete-on-road-to-ufc-japan|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150629003507/http://fightland.vice.com/blog/meet-the-eight-featherweights-who-will-compete-on-road-to-ufc-japan|url-status=dead|archive-date=June 29, 2015|title=Meet the eight featherweights who will compete on Road to UFC: Japan|publisher=fightland.vice.com|author=James Goyder|date=2015-06-26|access-date=2015-08-16}} The bout ended in a split draw, a first for the reality show series finale fights. During the show, the UFC announced that as a result of the draw both fighters would receive six-figure UFC contracts.{{cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/news/botched-road-ufc-final-ends-draw-both-fighters-081903521--mma.html|title=Botched Road to UFC Final ends in a draw, but both fighters awarded contracts|publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Staff|date=2015-09-27|access-date=2015-09-27}}\n\n[[Roan Carneiro]] was expected to face [[Gegard Mousasi]] at this event. However, Carneiro was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by [[Uriah Hall]].{{cite web|url=http://uk.ufc.com/news/Hall-Replaces-Carneiro-against-Mousasi|title=Hall Replaces Carneiro against Mousasi|publisher=uk.UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2015-08-19|date=2015-08-19}}\n\n[[Kiichi Kunimoto]] was expected to face [[Li Jingliang]] at this event. However, Kunimoto pulled out of the fight citing injury and was replaced by returning veteran [[Keita Nakamura]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/with-kunimoto-out-nakamura-keita-returns-to-face-li-jingliang-at-ufc-fight-night-75|title=With Kunimoto out, Nakamura Keita returns to face Li Jingliang at UFC Fight Night 75|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2015-08-28|date=2015-08-28}}\n\nA bout between [[Matt Hobar]] and [[Norifumi Yamamoto]] was expected to take place at this event. However, both fighters sustained injuries and were forced to pull out of the fight.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Fight-Night-Japan-injuries-scrap-Yamamoto-Hobar-fight|title= Injuries scrap Yamamoto-Hobar Japan fight|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2015-09-22|date=2015-09-22}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent 3]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Holohan vs. Smolka''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 76''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on 24 October 2015, at [[3Arena (Dublin)|3Arena]] in [[Dublin]], [[Republic of Ireland|Ireland]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Returns-To-Dublin-Saturday-October-24|title=UFC returns to Dublin on Saturday October 24|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2015-07-01|access-date=2015-07-29}} It featured [[Paddy Holohan]] and [[Louis Smolka]] as headliners. Smolka defeated Holohan via [[Rear naked choke|rear-naked choke]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the third [[UFC]] event in [[Dublin]], following [[UFC 93|''UFC 93: Franklin vs. Henderson'']] in January 2009 and ''[[UFC Fight Night: McGregor vs. Brandao]]'' in July 2014.\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a [[Lightweight (MMA)|lightweight]] bout between [[Dustin Poirier]] and [[Joseph Duffy (fighter)|Joseph Duffy]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/07/joseph-duffy-vs-dustin-poirier-headlines-ufc-fight-night-77-in-dublin|title=Joseph Duffy vs. Dustin Poirier headlines UFC Fight Night 76 in Dublin|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Tom Rooney|date=2015-07-29|access-date=2015-07-29}} However, Duffy was forced to pull out of the fight on 21 October, three days prior to the event, after sustaining a concussion during a sparring session earlier that week and the fight was scrapped. A [[Flyweight (MMA)|flyweight]] bout between [[Paddy Holohan]] and [[Louis Smolka]] was promoted to serve as the event's new headliner. It was the first three-rounds main event bout since ''[[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate Finale]]'' in November 2013.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/21/9587444/joseph-duffy-out-of-ufc-fight-night-76-paddy-holohan-vs-louis-smolka|title=Joseph Duffy out of UFC Fight Night 76, Paddy Holohan vs. Louis Smolka set as new main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2015-10-21|access-date=2015-10-21}}\n\nThe co-main event was expected to feature a [[Heavyweight (MMA)|heavyweight]] bout with potential [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] title implications between [[Stipe Miocic]] and [[Ben Rothwell]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/heavyweights-stipe-miocic-and-ben-rothwell-targeted-for-ufc-fight-night-76-in-dublin|title=Heavyweights Stipe Miocic and Ben Rothwell targeted for UFC Fight Night 76 in Dublin|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2015-08-12|access-date=2015-08-12}} However, Miocic pulled out of the fight on 14 October, ten days before the event, citing an injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/stipe-miocic-out-of-next-weeks-ufc-fight-night-76-co-headliner-with-ben-rothwell|title=Stipe Miocic out of next week's UFC Fight Night 76 co-headliner with Ben Rothwell|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2015-10-13|access-date=2015-10-13}} Rothwell was removed from the card the following day after the promotion deemed that a suitable opponent could not be arranged on short notice.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/14/9531469/ben-rothwell-removed-from-next-weeks-ufc-fight-night-dublin-card|title=Ben Rothwell removed from next week's UFC Fight Night Dublin card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2015-10-14|access-date=2015-10-14}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main vs. Romero","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2015}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Romero\n|image=Machdia_Romero_pre_sale.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2015|06|27}}\n|venue=[[Seminole Hard Rock Hotel and Casino Hollywood|Seminole Hard Rock Hotel and Casino]]\n|city=[[Hollywood, Florida]]\n|attendance={{nts|5604}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/ufc-fight-night-70-draws-sellout-crowd-of-5604|title=UFC Fight Night 70 draws sellout crowd of vs. McGregor]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Romero''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 70''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on June 27, 2015, at the [[Seminole Hard Rock Hotel and Casino Hollywood|Seminole Hard Rock Hotel and Casino]] in [[Hollywood, Florida]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Summer-UFC-Events-Secured-for-Florida-and-Nevada|title=Summer UFC Events Secured for Florida and Nevada|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-05-15|access-date=2015-05-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally expected to be held at the [[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]] in [[São Paulo]], [[Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/4/14/8417343/tuf-brazil-4-finale-set-for-june-27-in-sao-paulo|title=TUF: Brazil 4 Finale set for June 27 in Sao Paulo|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-04-14|access-date=2015-04-16}} However, on May 15, the event was moved to the [[Seminole Hard Rock Hotel and Casino Hollywood|Seminole Hard Rock Hotel and Casino]] in [[Hollywood, Florida]].\n\nThe fight card was also expected to feature the lightweight and bantamweight finals of ''[[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 4]]''.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/5/15/8610043/lyoto-machida-vs-yoel-romero-set-as-new-main-event-for-tuf-brazil-4|title=Lyoto Machida vs. Yoel Romero set as new main event for TUF Brazil: 4 Finale in Miami|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-05-15|access-date=2015-05-15}} This was the first time an Ultimate Fighter Brazil Finale event would be held outside of Brazil. However, as the event approached, several international fighters experienced travel restrictions due to technical issues within the [[Bureau of Consular Affairs]] division of the [[U.S. State Department]] which produces [[visa (document)|travel visas]]. The issue led to a major altering of the card as multiple fights were postponed.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/visa-issues-could-force-several-brazilian-fighters-out-of-ufc-fight-night-70|title=Visa issues could force several Brazilian fighters out of UFC Fight Night 70|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-06-18|access-date=2015-06-18}}{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Fight-Night-Loses-SilvaStory-TUF-Brazil-4-Finals-from-June-27-Card-in-Florida-87917|title=UFC Fight Night loses Silva-Story, 'TUF: Brazil 4' Finals from June 27 Card in Florida|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2015-06-19|access-date=2015-06-19}}\n\nThe lightweight and bantamweight finals of ''The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 4'', was instead contested on August 1, 2015 at [[UFC 190]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/6/19/8811849/visa-issues-forces-changes-at-ufc-fight-night-70-ufc-190|title=Visa issues forces changes at UFC Fight Night 70, UFC 190|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-06-19|access-date=2015-06-19}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Rick Story]] and [[Erick Silva]] was expected to serve as the main event.{{cite web|url=http://cagepages.com/2015/04/22/rick-story-vs-erick-silva-headlines-the-ultimate-fighter-brazil-4-finale-in-sao-paulo/|title=Rick Story vs. Erick Silva headlines The Ultimate Fighter Brazil 4 Finale in Sao Paulo|publisher=cagepages.com|author=Sean Bio|date=April 22, 2015|access-date=April 22, 2015}} However, after the initial venue change, the bout was moved to a co-featured slot and the event was headlined by a middleweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight champion]] [[Lyoto Machida]] and [[Wrestling at the 2000 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 85 kg|2000 Summer Olympics silver medalist]] in [[freestyle wrestling]] [[Yoel Romero]]. Also affected by the visa issues, Silva was pulled from the event and his bout against Story was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Oliveira]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/6/24/8839335/rick-story-vs-erick-silva-to-serve-as-ufc-saskatoon-co-main-event|title=Rick Story vs. Erick Silva to serve as UFC Saskatoon co-main event; other fights added to card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2015-06-24|access-date=2015-06-24}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Nikita Krylov]] and [[Marcos Rogério de Lima]] was briefly linked to [[UFC Fight Night 69]]. However, the pairing was expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://themmareport.com/2015/06/nikita-krylov-vs-marcos-rogerio-de-lima-moved-to-ufc-fight-night-70/|title=Nikita Krylov vs. Marcos Rogerio de Lima moved to UFC Fight Night 70 |publisher=themmareport.com|author=Staff|date=2015-06-09|access-date=2015-06-09}} Subsequently, the pairing was postponed again as de Lima faced visa issues.\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Rani Yahya]] and [[Masanori Kanehara]] also planned for the event was shifted to the [[UFC Fight Night 71]] event on July 15, 2015.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/visa-issues-force-massive-shuffling-at-next-weeks-ufc-fight-night-70-event|title=Visa issues force massive shuffling at next week's UFC Fight Night 70 event |publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-06-19|access-date=2015-06-19}}\n\n[[Chas Skelly]] was briefly linked to a bout with [[Hacran Dias]] at the event. However, Skelly was pulled from the card due to illness and was replaced by [[Levan Makashvili]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/levan-makashvili-replaces-chas-skelly-at-ufc-fight-night-70-in-florida|title=Levan Makashvili replaces Chas Skelly at UFC Fight Night 70 in Florida|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-06-01|access-date=2015-06-01}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Viscardi Andrade]] and Andreas Ståhl was scrapped as Ståhl suffered an injury in the week leading up to the event.\n\nPromotional newcomer Lewis Gonzalez missed weight on his first attempt at the weigh-ins, coming in 3 lb overweight at 174 lb. After having made no attempts to cut further, he was fined 20 percent of his fight purse, which went to [[Leandro Silva (mixed martial artist)|Leandro Silva]].{{cite web|url=http://411mania.com/mma/ufc-fight-night-70-weigh-in-results/|title=UFC Fight Night 70 weigh-in results|publisher=411mania.com|author=Jeffrey Harris|date=2015-06-26|access-date=2015-06-27|archive-date=2015-06-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150629195626/http://411mania.com/mma/ufc-fight-night-70-weigh-in-results/|url-status=dead}}\n\nIn a controversial post-fight interview, [[Yoel Romero]] seemed to express disagreement with the [[Obergefell v. Hodges]] supreme court ruling. However, during the post fight press conference and subsequent interviews the following day, Romero denied referring to gay marriage and apologized for his comments while maintaining that they were misunderstood and taken out of context.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/13162486/ufc-yoel-romero-denies-making-anti-gay-remark|title=Yoel Romero apologizes but denies making anti-gay 5 at MGM Grand in Las Vegas|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2015-06-03|access-date=2015-06-24}}\n\n==Background==\nInitially, a [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Daniel Cormier]] and top contender [[Alexander Gustafsson]] was linked as a possible event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/07/daniel-cormier-vs-alexander-gustafsson-was-targeted-for-september-but-injury-will-delay|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Alexander Gustafsson was targeted for September, but injury will delay|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2015-07-05|access-date=2015-07-05}} Although never officially announced by the UFC, the bout between Cormier and Gustafsson would not take place at this event and would instead be postponed and moved to [[UFC 192]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/07/daniel-cormier-vs-alexander-gustafsson-booked-as-ufc-192-headliner-in-houston|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Alexander Gustafsson booked as UFC 192 headliner in Houston|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2015-07-18|access-date=2015-07-18}}\n\nDuring the live [[Fox Sports 1]] broadcast of [[UFC Fight Night: Mir vs. Duffee]], it was confirmed that a [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson (fighter)|Demetrious Johnson]] and top contender [[John Dodson (fighter)|John Dodson]] would headline the event.{{cite news|url=https://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-191-headlined-by-demetrious-johnson-vs-john-dodson-2-rumble-johnson-returns-071515|title=UFC 191 headlined by Demetrious Johnson vs. John Dodson 2, 'Rumble' Johnson returns|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2015-07-15|access-date=2015-07-15}} Their first fight at [[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Dodson]] ended in a unanimous decision victory for Johnson.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Demetrious-Johnson-Outduels-John-Dodson-Keeps-Flyweight-Title-at-UFC-on-Fox-6-49417|title=Demetrious Johnson outduels John Dodson, keeps flyweight title at UFC on Fox 6|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Brian Knapp|date=2013-01-26|access-date=2013-01-26}}\n\nThe co-main event featured a heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight champions]] [[Andrei Arlovski]] and [[Frank Mir]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/Finally-Mir-vs-Arlovski-Set-for-UFC-191|title=Finally – Mir vs. Arlovski set for UFC 191|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-08-01|access-date=2015-08-02}}\n\n[[Anthony Kewoa Johnson|Anthony Johnson]] was originally expected to face [[Jan Błachowicz]] on the card. However, Johnson was pulled from the bout on July 30 in favor of a fight with [[Jimi Manuwa]] at the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-anthony-johnson-now-faces-jimi-manuwa-at-ufc-191-in-las-vegas-073015|title=Anthony Johnson now faces Jimi Manuwa at UFC 191 in Las Vegas|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2015-07-30|access-date=2015-07-30}} In turn, Błachowicz faced [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn|The Ultimate Fighter 19]] light heavyweight winner [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]].{{cite web|url=https://themmareport.com/2015/07/jan-blachowicz-vs-corey-anderson-set-for-ufc-191/|title=Jan Blachowicz vs. Corey Anderson set for UFC 191|publisher=themmareport.com|author=Jason Floyd|date=2015-07-30|access-date=2015-07-30}}\n\n[[Raquel Pennington]] was initially expected to face [[Leslie Smith (fighter)|Leslie Smith]] at [[UFC 192]]. However, Smith was forced to pull out of the event due to injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmanytt.se/galor/ufc/leslie-smith-drar-sig-ur-ufc-192-och-matchen-mot-raquel-pennington/|title=Leslie Smith drar sig ur UFC 192 och matchen mot Raquel Pennington|publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Denniz Löfgren Hasyilmaz|date=2015-08-05|access-date=2015-08-05}} Subsequently, Pennington was pulled from the card entirely in favor of a bout with former title challenger [[Liz Carmouche]] at this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/liz-carmouche-meets-raquel-pennington-at-ufc-191-in-las-vegas|title=Liz Carmouche meets Raquel Pennington at UFC 191 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-08-12|access-date=2015-08-12}} In turn, Carmouche pulled out of the fight just days after being added, citing injury and was replaced by [[Jéssica Andrade]]. This fight was a rematch, as Andrade beat Pennington via split decision at [[UFC 171]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/jessica-andrade-in-for-liz-carmouche-to-rematch-raquel-pennington-at-ufc-191|title=Jessica Andrade in for Liz Carmouche to rematch Raquel Pennington at UFC Fight Night: Holohan vs. Smolka]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 192: Cormier vs. Gustafsson''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on October 3, 2015, at the [[Toyota Center]] in [[Houston, Texas]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-192-Headed-to-Houston-Fight-Night-Cards-Announced-for-Nashville-San-Diego-83337|title=UFC 192 headed to Houston|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2015-03-15|access-date=2015-03-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the fourth that the promotion has hosted in Houston, and the first since [[UFC 166]] in 2013.\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Daniel Cormier]] and top contender [[Alexander Gustafsson]]. The bout was initially linked as the event headliner for [[UFC 191]]. However, the bout was postponed to this event as a result of Cormier's knee injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/07/daniel-cormier-vs-alexander-gustafsson-booked-as-ufc-192-headliner-in-houston|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Alexander Gustafsson booked as UFC 192 headliner in Houston|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2015-07-18|access-date=2015-07-18}}\n\n[[Leslie Smith (fighter)|Leslie Smith]] was expected to face [[Raquel Pennington]] at this event. However, Smith was forced to pull out of the event due to injury and Pennington was rebooked against a separate opponent at another event.{{cite web|url=http://mmanytt.se/galor/ufc/leslie-smith-drar-sig-ur-ufc-192-och-matchen-mot-raquel-pennington/|title=Leslie Smith drar sig ur UFC 192 och matchen mot Raquel Pennington|publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Denniz Löfgren Hasyilmaz|date=2015-08-05|access-date=2015-08-05}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/liz-carmouche-meets-raquel-pennington-at-ufc-191-in-las-vegas|title=Liz Carmouche meets Raquel Pennington at UFC 191 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-08-12|access-date=2015-08-12}}\n\n[[Anthony Hamilton (fighter)|Anthony Hamilton]] was expected to face [[Derrick Lewis]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://themmareport.com/2015/08/ufc-191-adds-andre-fili-vs-clay-collard-joe-riggs-vs-ron-stallings/ |title=UFC 191 adds Andre Fili vs. Clay Collard|publisher=themmareport.com |author=Staff|date=2015-08-06|access-date=2015-08-06}} However, Hamilton was pulled from the bout and replaced by [[Viktor Pešta]].{{cite web|url=http://blog.chron.com/sportsupdate/2015/08/ufc-192-hamilton-out-viktor-pesta-faces-derrick-lewis/|title=UFC 192: Hamilton out, Viktor Pesta faces Derrick Lewis|publisher=blog.chron.com|author=Jeremy Botter|date=2015-08-20|access-date=2015-08-20}}\n\n[[Johny Hendricks]] was set to face [[Tyron Woodley]] in the co-main event. However, Hendricks pulled out of the fight on the day of the weigh-ins due to a [[kidney stone]] and intestinal blockage suffered during his weight cut. Therefore, the fight was canceled.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/ufc-scraps-saturdays-johny-hendricks-vs-tyron-woodley-ufc-192-co-headliner|title=UFC scraps Saturday's Johny Hendricks vs. Tyron Woodley UFC 192 co-headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-10-02|access-date=2015-10-02}}\n\n[[Francisco Treviño]] missed weight on his first attempt at the weigh ins, coming in at 160 lb. He was given additional time to make the weight limit, but made no attempts to cut further. Instead, he was fined 20 percent of his fight purse, which went to [[Sage Northcutt]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/ufc-192-live-and-official-weigh-in-results|title=UFC 192 weigh-in results: Cormier and Gustafsson on point for title showdown|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-10-02|access-date=2015-10-02}}\n\nOn October 15, it was announced that Treviño tested positive for [[marijuana]] in post-fight tests. If Treviño was found by the [[Texas Department of Licensing and Regulation]]'s enforcement division to have violated its rules, he would face a one-year probated suspension, in which he can still fight provided he does not break any more rules, or a full one-year suspension. He also faced up to a $5,000 fine.{{Cite web|last=Raimondi|first=Marc|date=2015-10-15|title=Trevino pops for weed after Northcutt loss|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/15/9540741/francisco-trevino-tests-positive-for-marijuana-after-loss-to-sage|access-date=2020-09-02|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nThis event also included the only fight as of 2026 that [[Islam Makhachev]] has lost, losing to [[Adriano Martins]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Night: Albert Tumenov''' and '''Adriano Martins'''\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2015 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Texas]]\n[[Category:October 2015 sports events in the United States]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC 192"}]},{"pageid":47434468,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 193","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2015}}\n{{Use dmy dates|date=July 2016}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 193: Rousey vs. Holm\n|image=UFC 193 promo.jpeg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2015|11|14}}{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/events/UFC-193-Rousey-vs-Holm-42239|title=UFC 193 – Rousey vs Holm|website=Sherdog.com|access-date=31 December 2015}}\n|venue=[[Docklands Stadium]]\n|city=[[Melbourne]], Australia\n|attendance=56,214{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/15/9737660/ufc-193-crowd-of-56214-sets-company-attendance-record|title=UFC 193 crowd of 56,214 sets company attendance record|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=17 January 2017|access-date=14 November 2015}}\n|gate= $6,800,000\n|buyrate=1,100,000{{Cite web |url=http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |title=Archived copy |access-date=8 March 2016 |archive-date=27 June 2011 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110627142203/http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |url-status=dead }}\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Belfort vs. Henderson 3]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night 78|The Ultimate Fighter Latin America 2 Finale: Magny vs. Gastelum]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 193: Rousey vs. Holm''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on November 14, 2015,{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/events/UFC-193-Rousey-vs-Holm-42239|title=UFC 193 – Rousey vs Holm|website=Sherdog.com|access-date=31 December 2015}} at [[Docklands Stadium|Etihad Stadium]] in [[Melbourne]], Australia. It was the most attended UFC event in the history of the company at the time, with 56,214 people filling the stadium until [[UFC 243]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/15/9737660/ufc-193-crowd-of-56214-sets-company-attendance-record |title=UFC 193 crowd of 56,214 sets company attendance record |date=15 November 2015 |publisher=MMA Fighting |access-date=31 December 2015}} Although [[Ronda Rousey]] was heavily favored to defeat [[Holly Holm]], Holm gave Rousey her first career defeat.\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the promotion has held in [[Melbourne]]. [[Docklands Stadium]] was the third [[stadium|stadium venue]] to host a UFC event after [[UFC 129]] at the [[Rogers Centre]] in [[Canada]] and [[UFC on Fox: Gustafsson vs. Johnson]] held at [[Tele2 Arena]] in [[Sweden]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/8/19/9177503/melbournes-etihad-stadium-set-to-host-ufc-193|title=Melbourne's Etihad Stadium set to host UFC 193|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=19 August 2015|access-date=19 August 2015}}\n\nThe event was initially expected to be headlined by a [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Robbie Lawler]] and [[Carlos Condit]], a former [[List of World Extreme Cagefighting champions#Welterweight Championship|WEC Welterweight Champion]] and former interim UFC Welterweight Champion. However, the bout was removed from the event after Lawler suffered a thumb injury stemming back from his high school wrestling career.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/robbie-lawler-reveals-15-year-old-injury-forced-carlos-condit-postponement|title=Robbie Lawler reveals 15-year-old injury forced Carlos Condit postponement|publisher=mmaweekly.com|date=22 October 2015|access-date=30 October 2015}} Subsequently, a [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Ronda Rousey]] and former 18-time [[boxing]] world champion in three weight classes [[Holly Holm]], originally scheduled for [[UFC 195]], was announced as the new headliner for this event.{{cite news|url=https://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-193-robbie-lawler-carlos-condit-thumb-injury-ronda-rousey-holly-holm-rescheduled-082915|title=Robbie Lawler suffers thumb injury, fight with Carlos Condit expected to be rescheduled|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=29 August 2015|access-date=29 August 2015}} The Lawler/Condit bout was eventually rescheduled to UFC 195.\n\nCo-featured at the event was a [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] and [[Valérie Létourneau]].{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/champ-joanna-jedrzejczyk-meets-valerie-letourneau-at-ufc-193-rousey-vs-holm|title=Champ Joanna Jedrzejczyk meets Valerie Letourneau at 'UFC 193: Rousey vs. Holm'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=4 September 2015|access-date=4 September 2015}}\n\nBrazilian fighters [[William Macario]] and Ricardo Abreu were expected to face [[Brendan O'Reilly (fighter)|Brendan O'Reilly]] and [[Dan Kelly (fighter)|Daniel Kelly]] respectively at the event. However, both fighters pulled out of their bouts in late September citing injury and were replaced by [[James Moontasri]] and Steve Montgomery.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/after-william-macario-injury-brendan-oreilly-gets-new-ufc-193-opponent|title=After William Macario injury, Brendan O'Reilly gets new UFC 193 opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=21 September 2015|access-date=21 September 2015}}{{cite news|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Steve-Montgomery-Replaces-Ricardo-Abreu-Meets-Daniel-Kelly-at-UFC-193-92871|title=Steve Montgomery replaces Ricardo Abreu, meets Daniel Kelly at UFC 193|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=23 September 2015|access-date=23 September 2015}} In turn, O'Reilly pulled out of his bout on 6 November citing an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer Anton Zafir.{{cite web|url=http://www.heraldsun.com.au/sport/ufc-193-zafir-gets-his-chance-after-oreilly-forced-out/news-story/e34b8250ff3d81dab998d791477fde0f|title=UFC 193: Zafir gets his chance after O'Reilly forced out|publisher=heraldsun.com.au|author=Alex Oates|date=6 November 2015|access-date=6 November 2015}}\n\n[[Michael Bisping]] was expected to face [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] at the event. However, Bisping pulled out of the bout on 30 September citing an elbow injury and was replaced by [[Uriah Hall]].{{cite web|url=https://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/michael-bisping-injured-out-of-ufc-193-093015|title=Michael Bisping injured, out of UFC 193|work=foxsports.com|author=Elias Cepeda|date=30 September 2015|access-date=30 September 2015}}{{cite web|url=https://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-uriah-hall-replaces-michael-bisping-faces-robert-whittaker-at-ufc-193-100115|title=Uriah Hall replaces Michael Bisping, faces Robert Whittaker at UFC 193|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=1 October 2015|access-date=1 October 2015}}\n\n==Rousey vs. Holm fight==\nRonda Rousey began the Women's Bantamweight bout aggressively. Holly Holm, with her boxing ability, and from her [[southpaw stance]], tagged Rousey repeatedly with her left hand while controlling the distance. Rousey attempted several [[Clinch fighting|clinches]] through the round, including an attempted [[armbar]], but none had a decisive outcome. By the end of the first round, Rousey was bloodied and breathing heavily, with commentators describing the round as the first she had ever lost in her MMA career.{{cite web|last1=Martin|first1=Todd|title=UFC 193 complete results: Holly Holm stuns Ronda Rousey|url=https://www.latimes.com/sports/boxing/la-sp-sn-ufc-193-live-updates-ronda-rousey-vs-holly-holm-20151114-story.html|work=Los Angeles Times|date=15 November 2015|access-date=15 November 2015}}{{cite web| url=https://www.smh.com.au/sport/ufc-193-ronda-rousey-loses-to-holly-holm-20151115-gkzhj6.html| title=UFC 193: Ronda Rousey loses to Holly Holm by knockout| author=Matt Murnane| work=[[The Sydney Morning Herald]]| date=16 November 2015| access-date=16 November 2015}}\n\nRound two followed a similar pattern to the first, with Rousey trying to close the distance but being unable to, frustrated by Holm's stick and move strategy. Rousey stumbled forward after taking a straight left to her chin, and while rising, Holm connected with a kick to the side of Rousey's neck, knocking her out. Holm pounced on the fallen Rousey, causing referee [[Herb Dean]] to end the bout.{{cite web|last1=Reddick|first1=Jay|title=Rousey knocked out; Holm wins UFC title|url=http://www.orlandosentinel.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/os-ufc-193-results-ronda-rousey-holly-holm-20151114-post.html|publisher=Orlando Sentinel|access-date=15 November 2015}}\n\nMMA website [[Sherdog]] called the fight its Upset of the Year for 2015.{{cite web|title=SHERDOG.COM'S 2015 UPSET OF THE YEAR Holm vs. Rousey|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/articles/1/Sherdogcoms-2015-Upset-of-the-Year-97613|website=Sherdog.com}}\n\n==Jędrzejczyk vs. Létourneau fight==\nIn round one, both fighters exchanged punches at each other. Jędrzejczyk then attempted a combination via a kick, however Létourneau was able to grab Jędrzejczyk's leg and take her down. Jędrzejczyk also landed a front kick to the face that made Létourneau walk unsteadily for a few seconds.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/15/9736630/joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-valerie-letourneau-full-fight-video-highlights-ufc-193 |title=Joanna Jedrzejczyk vs. Valerie Letourneau full fight video highlights |publisher=MMA Fighting |date=15 November 2015 |access-date=31 December 2015}} The two fighters continued exchanging shots at one another for the rest of the round.{{cite web|last1=Muehlhausen|first1=Steven|title=UFC 193 Results: Joanna Jędrzejczyk retains her title in five-round slugfest|work=Sporting News|date=15 November 2015|url=http://www.sportingnews.com/sport-news/4661115-ufc-193-results-joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-valerie-letourneau-rousey-vs-holm-video-analysis|access-date=16 November 2015|archive-date=17 November 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20151117022700/http://www.sportingnews.com/sport-news/4661115-ufc-193-results-joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-valerie-letourneau-rousey-vs-holm-video-analysis|url-status=dead}}\n\nJust like the first round, both fighters began the second by exchanging numerous punches. Jędrzejczyk attempted a kick on Létourneau, but Létourneau was able to block the kick, allowing her to punch Jędrzejczyk numerous times. Létourneau later had Jędrzejczyk pinned against the cage, however Jędrzejczyk was able to throw numerous elbow strikes at Létourneau. They continued to exchange shots throughout the round, with Jędrzejczyk throwing more combinations towards the end of the round.\n\nIn round three, Létourneau began by throwing a combination. Jędrzejczyk then proceeded to throw some leg kicks, as well as front kicks. Létourneau was showing a bit of tiredness at this point, which Jędrzejczyk took advantage of. Jędrzejczyk chopped Létourneau's lead leg. Along with this, both Létourneau's right eye and leg were swelling. The round finished with Jędrzejczyk doing both a combination and a leg kick.\n\nJędrzejczyk started round four with a left jab on Létourneau. Létourneau fought back, hitting a couple of punches and kicks to the body. Despite this, Jędrzejczyk continued to hit left jabs on Létourneau. Jędrzejczyk hit numerous punches throughout the rest of the round, including numerous combinations towards the end of the round.\n\nIn the final round, both fighters went straight at each other. Jędrzejczyk did numerous leg kicks on Létourneau. Létourneau was able to hit more shots on Jędrzejczyk, however, Jędrzejczyk was throwing various different shots.\n\nThe judges scored the fight 49–46, 49–46, and 50–45, unanimously in favor of Jędrzejczyk.{{cite news |first=Brett |last=Okamoto |date=15 November 2015 |title=Joanna Jędrzejczyk defeats Valerie Létourneau by unanimous decision |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/14133352/joanna-jedrzejczyk-defeats-valerie-letourneau-unanimous-decision-retain-strawweight-title |website=ESPN |access-date=16 November 2015 }}\n\n==Other fights==\nIn a rematch of heavyweights, [[Mark Hunt]] finished [[Antônio Silva (fighter)|Antônio Silva]] via TKO in round one with a right straight that knocked him to his knees and a follow-up left hook.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/14131972/mark-hunt-knocks-antonio-silva-rematch|title=Mark Hunt knocks out Antonio Silva in rematch|work=ESPN.com|date=15 November 2015|access-date=17 November 2015}}\n\n[[Kyle Noke]] defeated [[Peter Sobotta]] via TKO in round one of their welterweight bout with a front kick and a flurry of follow up punches.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/ufc-193-results-thundering-kyle-noke-front-kick-tko-blasts-peter-sobotta-into-agony|title=UFC 193 results: Thundering Kyle Noke front kick TKO blasts Peter Sobotta into agony|work=MMAjunkie|date=15 November 2015|access-date=17 November 2015}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n| Women's Bantamweight\n|[[Holly web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-193-Bonuses-Holly-Holm-Nets-Extra-36100000-Ronda-Rousey-Kyle-Noke-Get-3650K-95719|title=UFC 193 Bonuses: Holly Holm nets extra $100,000; Ronda Rousey, Holly Holm''' and '''Kyle Noke'''\n\n==Reactions==\nUFC president [[Dana White]] commented on the Rousey vs. Holm fight, stating \"This is a fight. This is what happens\", then going on to state \"All the greats eventually go down one day\".{{cite web|last1=Shelburne|first1=Ramona|title=No Longer Invincible, but Ronda Rousey is Hardly Finished|date=15 November 2015|url=https://www.espn.com/espnw/news-commentary/story/_/id/14135662/no-longer-invincible-ronda-rousey-hardly-finished|publisher=ESPN|access-date=16 November 2015}} White also said that \"there will absolutely be a rematch.\" [[Joe Rogan]], color-commentator for the UFC, stated Holm's win is \"by far the biggest upset in UFC history.\"{{cite web|last1=Doyle|first1=Ricky|title=Dana White: Ronda Rousey 'Bummed Out And Depressed' After UFC 193 Loss|date=15 November 2015|url=https://nesn.com/2015/11/dana-white-ronda-rousey-bummed-out-and-depressed-after-ufc-193-loss/|publisher=NESN|access-date=16 November 2015}}\n\n==See also==\n{{Portal|Australia|Martial $10,006,249{{cite web|url=http://boxing.nv.gov/results/Top_MMA_Gates|title=Top MMA Gates|date=September 16, 2016|publisher=Nevada State Athletic Commission}}\n|previousevent=[[The Ultimate Fighter: Nevada.{{cite news|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/mma-cagewriter/dana-white--aldo-mcgregor-set-for-dec--12-at-mgm-grand-in-las-vegas-004231499.html|title=Dana White: Aldo-McGregor set for Dec. 12 at MGM Grand in Las Vegas|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2015-08-10|access-date=2015-08-10}}{{cite news|url=http://ufc.com/news/Aldo-vs-McGregor-Finds-a-Home-in-Vegas-on-Dec-12|title=Aldo vs. McGregor finds a home in Vegas on Dec. 12|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-08-11|access-date=2015-08-11}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally scheduled to take place on December 5. However, the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] had already been booked for an [[Andrea Bocelli]] concert, prompting the UFC to look at alternative venues such as the [[Mandalay Bay Events Center]]. The [[AT&T Stadium]] in [[Arlington, Texas]] was the most relevant venue under consideration, which would likely have made the event the highest attended in the promotion's history.{{cite news|url=http://www.dallasnews.com/sports/more-sports/headlines/20150716-report-att-stadium-being-looked-at-for-ufc-194-which-could-feature-fight-between-jose-aldo-conor-mcgregor.ece|title=Report: AT&T Stadium being looked at for UFC 194, which could feature fight between Jose Aldo, Conor McGregor and Prady who is Mcgregors best friend.|publisher=dallasnews.com|author=Staff|date=2015-07-16|access-date=2015-08-11}} Subsequently, on August 10, UFC president [[Dana White]] confirmed the event would take place at the MGM Grand Garden Arena, albeit one week later on December 12.\n[[File:José Aldo vs. Conor McGregor, UFC 189 World Tour London.jpg|300px|thumb|left|McGregor (right), Dana White (middle) and [[José Aldo]] (left) prior to the [[UFC 189]] press conference in March 2015.]]\nThis event marked the first time the UFC has ever scheduled three events in consecutive days, with all of them taking place in Las Vegas. Instead of the MGM Grand Garden Arena, the other two events were held at [[Cosmopolitan of Las Vegas|The Chelsea at The Cosmopolitan]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/9/4/9263143/ufc-to-run-three-las-vegas-events-in-span-of-three-days-in-december|title=UFC to run three Las Vegas events in span of three days in December|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-09-04|access-date=2015-09-04}}\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] unification bout between then champion [[José Aldo]] and then interim champion [[Conor McGregor]]. The bout was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 189]]. However, Aldo pulled out of the bout due to a rib injury and the event was instead headlined by an interim title bout between McGregor and [[Chad Mendes]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/jose-aldo-officially-out-of-ufc-189-chad-mendes-meets-conor-mcgregor-for-interim-belt|title=Jose Aldo officially out of UFC 189; Chad Mendes meets Conor McGregor for interim belt|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2015-06-30|access-date=2015-08-10}}\n\nThe co-main event featured a [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between then undefeated champion [[Chris Weidman]] and former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce Middleweight champion]] [[Luke Rockhold]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Weidman-vs-Rockhold-is-UFC-194-Co-Main-Event|title=Weidman vs. Rockhold is UFC 194 co-main event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-08-13|access-date=2015-08-13}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Ronaldo Souza]] and [[Yoel Romero]] was originally booked for [[UFC 184]] and later ''[[UFC on Fox: Machida vs. Rockhold]]''. However, the bout was cancelled both times due to Souza being struck with pneumonia{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/1/15/7555065/ufc-184-fight-card-jacare-souza-out-pneumonia-vs-yoel-romero-scratched|title='Jacare' Souza out with pneumonia, bout against Yoel Romero scratched from UFC 184|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2015-01-15|access-date=2015-08-17}} and Romero pulling out due to a ligament and meniscus tear in his knee, respectively.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/4/10/8384799/yoel-romero-injured-out-of-ufc-on-fox-15-bout-opposite-jacare-souza|title=Yoel Romero injured, out of UFC on FOX 15 bout opposite Jacare Souza|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Luke Thomas|date=2015-04-10|access-date=2015-08-17}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/08/yoel-romero-vs-ronaldo-jacare-souza-booked-for-ufc-194-in-december|title=Yoel Romero vs. Ronaldo 'Jacare' Souza booked for UFC 194 in December|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-08-17|access-date=2015-08-17}}\n\n[[Michelle Waterson]] was expected to face [[Tecia Torres]] at the event. However, Waterson pulled out of the bout on November 24 citing an injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/super-card-takes-a-hit-michelle-waterson-out-of-ufc-194-replacement-sought|title=Super card takes a hit: Michelle Waterson out of UFC 194|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp and John Morgan|date=2015-11-24|access-date=2015-11-24}} She was replaced by\npromotional newcomer [[Jocelyn Jones-Lybarger]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/rfa-champ-jocelyn-jones-lybarger-steps-in-against-tecia-torres-at-ufc-194|title=RFA champ Jocelyn Jones-Lybarger steps in against Tecia Torres at UFC 194|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-27|access-date=2015-11-27}}\n\nOn January 13, Romero was informed of a potential doping violation stemming from an out of competition test conducted by [[USADA]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Statement-on-Yoel-Romero-11216?nd=1|title=UFC statement on Yoel Romero|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-13|access-date=2016-01-13}} On February 8, Romero and his manager explained that he took a supplement after his fight and that supplement turned out to be contaminated. His team and USADA both sent out the supplement for testing and it came back that it contained a banned substance. That substance was not listed on the label, his manager said. They did not want to reveal the name of the supplement or the substance, because USADA was investigating the issue.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/8/10941272/manager-contaminated-supplement-led-to-yoel-romeros-failed-drug-test|title=Manager: Contaminated supplement led to Yoel Romero's failed drug test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-02-08|access-date=2016-02-09}} On March 23, it was announced that Romero would appeal his suspension. His team and USADA discussed a deal for a nine-month suspension for the fighter, but Romero preferred to go to arbitration. The typical USADA anti-doping violation suspension is two years.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/23/11295456/yoel-romero-to-appeal-usada-suspension-for-anti-doping-policy|title=Yoel Romero to appeal USADA suspension for anti-doping policy violation|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-03-23|access-date=2016-03-23}} Eventually on April 4, both parts reached an agreement for a six-month suspension. USADA revealed that Romero tested positive for [[ibutamoren]], a [[growth hormone]] release stimulator. The supplement was tested and the banned substance did indeed come up, even though it was not listed on the label. Romero announced that he'll pursue legal action against the supplement company within the next few weeks. The result of the fight was not disqualified or overturned.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/4/11364368/yoel-romero-gets-six-month-suspension-from-usada-for-failing-drug|title=Yoel Romero gets six-month suspension from USADA for failing drug test due to contaminated supplement|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-04|access-date=2016-04-04}}\n\n==McGregor vs. Aldo fight==\nBefore the opening of the first round, they refused to touch gloves and went immediately into combat.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/13/9994502/ufc-194-live-blog-jose-aldo-vs-conor-mcgregor|title=UFC 194 live blog: Jose Aldo vs. Conor McGregor|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Luke Thomas|date=2015-12-13|access-date=2015-12-13}} As the fight started, McGregor took control of the center of the octagon. When Aldo got within his range, McGregor threw a straight left hand to keep Aldo away. The punch did not hit its target. McGregor then followed with a leg kick that connected. As Aldo charged forward to connect with a right-left punch combination, McGregor moved backwards and connected with a left hook that landed on Aldo's chin and rendered him unconscious. Both punches connected, but McGregor's left punch landed first. As Aldo fell to the ground, McGregor connected with two [[Strike (attack)#Hammerfist|hammer fist]]s until the referee ([[John McCarthy (referee)|Big John McCarthy]]) stepped in to stop the fight. When the fight was over, McGregor leaped atop the fence and celebrated his victory. Within a minute, Aldo regained consciousness and was on his feet. The bout lasted 13 seconds. The knockout was the fastest finish in UFC title fight history. “Again, nobody can take that left-hand shot,” said McGregor in the post-fight interview. “[Aldo's] powerful and he's fast. But precision beats power, and timing beats speed. And that’s what you saw there.”{{cite web|url=https://www.washingtonpost.com/sports/conor-mcgregor-caps-ufc-194-by-knocking-out-jose-aldo-in-13-seconds/2015/12/13/ce7eba42-a155-11e5-8728-1af6af208198_story.html|title=Conor McGregor caps UFC 194 by knocking out Jose Aldo in 13 seconds|work=washingtonpost.com|author=Jeff Wagenheim|date=2015-12-13|access-date=2015-12-13}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Marcio Alexandre Jr.: $12,000\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2015 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:2015 in sports in Nevada]]\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship events]]\n[[Category:2015 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:December 2015 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship events at MGM Grand Garden Arena]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC 194"}]},{"pageid":47517587,"ns":0,"title":"UFC on Fox: dos Anjos vs. Cowboy 2","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2015}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC on Fox: dos Anjos vs. Cowboy 2\n|image=UFC on FOX 17 pre sale.jpeg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2015|12|19}}\n|venue=[[Amway Center]]\n|city=[[Orlando, Florida]]\n|attendance= 14,459{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-on-fox-17-draws-reported-14459-attendance-for-1-71-million-gate|title=UFC on FOX 17 draws reported 14,459 attendance for $1.71 million gate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=December 19, 2015}}\n|buyrate= \n|gate=$1,710,000\n|previousevent=[[UFC 194|UFC 194: Aldo vs. McGregor]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 195|UFC 195: Lawler vs. Condit]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: dos Anjos vs. Cowboy 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 17''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on December 19, 2015, at the [[Amway Center]] in [[Orlando, Florida]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Dos-Anjos-vs-Cerrone-II-Headlines-on-Dec-19|title=dos Anjos vs. Cerrone 2 headlines on Dec. 19|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-08-13|access-date=2015-08-13}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and top contender [[Donald Cerrone]]. Dos Anjos won their first fight at [[UFC Fight Night: Condit vs. Kampmann 2]] by unanimous decision.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/8/29/4670880/ufc-fight-night-27-results-recap-rafael-dos-anjos-vs-donald-cerrone-review-analysis-mma|title=UFC Fight Night 27 results recap: Rafael dos Anjos vs. Donald Cerrone fight review and analysis|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Michael Drahota|date=2013-08-29|access-date=2015-08-13}}\n\n[[Germaine de Randamie]] was scheduled to face [[Sarah Kaufman]] at the event. However, De Randamie pulled out of the fight on December 3 citing an injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/germaine-de-randamie-injured-out-of-ufc-on-fox-17-bout-with-sarah-kaufman|title=Germaine de Randamie injured, out of UFC on FOX 17 bout with Sarah Kaufman|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-03|access-date=2015-12-14}} On December 11, she was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Valentina Shevchenko]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/de-Randamie-out--Shevchenko-to-debut-against-Kaufman|title=de Randamie out, Shevchenko to debut against Kaufman|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-11|access-date=2015-12-11}}\n\n[[Charles Oliveira]] missed weight on his first attempt at the weigh ins, coming in at 150.5 lb. He was given additional time to make the weight limit, but made no attempts to cut further. Instead, he was fined 20 percent of his fight purse, which went to [[Myles Jury]]. This marked the third time that Oliveira missed weight in his UFC career.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-on-fox-17-live-and-official-weigh-in-results-1|title=UFC on FOX 17 official weigh-in results: Some confusion atop the scale|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-18|access-date=2015-12-18}}\n\nBy reporters and UFC fans alike, UFC on Fox 17 is regarded as one of the greatest cards that the UFC has ever offered on broadcast television. Future UFC stars such as [[Nate Diaz]] and [[Vicente Luque]], and future champions [[Valentina Shevchenko]], [[Charles Oliveira]], and [[Francis Ngannou]] were all victorious on this card. Additionally, [[Kamaru Usman]] defeated [[Leon Edwards]] by unanimous decision; the two met in a rematch for the [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] on August 20, 2022, headlining [[UFC 278]].{{cite web | url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/34077245/kamaru-usman-defend-ufc-title-leon-edwards-ufc-278-aug-20 | title=Usman-Edwards title bout booked for UFC 278 | date=12 June 2022 }} Edwards won by knockout in the fifth round.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Rafael dos Anjos]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Donald Cerrone]]\n|TKO (body kick and punches)\n|1\n|1:06\n|For the [[UFC Lightweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Alistair Overeem]]\n|def.\n|[[Junior dos Santos]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|4:43\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Nate Diaz]]\n|def.\n|[[Michael Johnson (fighter)|Michael Johnson]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Karolina Kowalkiewicz]]\n|def.\n|[[Randa Markos]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (150.5 lb)\n|[[Charles Oliveira]]\n|def.\n|[[Myles Jury]]\n|Submission (guillotine choke)\n|1\n|3:04\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Nate Marquardt]]\n|def.\n|[[C. Fight Night: Henderson vs. Masvidal\n|image=UFC Seoul 2015 poster.png\n|promotion=Ultimate Fighting Championship\n|date= {{start date|2015|11|28}}\n|venue=[[Olympic Gymnastics Arena]]\n|city=[[Seoul]], [[South Korea]]\n|attendance=12,156{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/ufc-fight-night-79-draws-reported-12156-attendance-for-first-event-in-south-korea|title=UFC Fight Night 79 draws reported 12,156 attendance for first event in South Korea|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-28|access-date=2015-11-28}}\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[The Ultimate vs. VanZant]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Henderson vs. Masvidal''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 79''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on November 28, 2015, at [[Olympic Gymnastics Arena]] in [[Seoul]], [[South Korea]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/5/16/8615707/ufc-announces-debut-south-korean-event-will-be-held-on-nov-28|title=UFC announces debut South Korean event will be held on Nov. 28|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2015-05-16|access-date=2015-05-16}}{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/Star-Studded-Main-Card-Announced-For-Inaugural-UFC-Seoul-Event|title=Main Card Announced For First Seoul Event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2015-08-23|access-date=2015-08-24}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the organization hosted in [[South Korea]].\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[List of World Extreme Cagefighting champions#Lightweight Championship|WEC]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Benson Henderson]] and former top contender [[Thiago Alves (fighter)|Thiago Alves]] was expected to headline the event. However, on November 14, Alves pulled out the bout due to a broken rib and was replaced by [[Jorge Masvidal]], who was originally scheduled to face [[Dong Hyun Kim]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/14/9734426/jorge-masvidal-in-for-injured-thiago-alves-vs-ben-henderson-at-ufc|title=Jorge Masvidal in for injured Thiago Alves vs. Ben Henderson at UFC Fight Night 79|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-11-14|access-date=2015-11-14}} Kim in turn faced Dominic Waters.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/dong-hyun-kim-draws-dominic-waters-at-ufc-fight-night-79|title=Dong Hyun Kim draws Dominic Waters at UFC Fight Night 79|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-15|access-date=2015-11-15}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Doo Ho Choi]] and [[Sam Sicilia]] was originally booked for [[UFC 173]] and later [[UFC Fight Night: Mir vs. Duffee]]. However, Choi pulled out both times due to an injury and undisclosed reasons, respectively.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2014/05/sam-sicilia-now-meets-newcomer-aaron-phillips-at-ufc-173-in-las-vegas|title=Sam Sicilia now meets newcomer Aaron Phillips at UFC 173 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2014-05-05|access-date=2015-11-25}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/doo-ho-choi-forced-out-of-featured-ufc-fight-night-71-prelim|title=Doo Ho Choi forced out of featured UFC Fight Night 71 prelim|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-06-28|access-date=2015-11-25}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/doo-ho-choi-vs-sam-sicilia-added-to-ufc-fight-night-79|title=Doo Ho Choi vs. Sam Sicilia added to UFC Fight Night 79|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-09-01|access-date=2015-11-25}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between the [[List of Pride Fighting Championships champions#Tournament Winners|2006 Pride Open-Weight Grand Prix champion]] and [[K-1 World Grand Prix|2012 K-1 World Grand Prix champion]] [[Mirko Filipović]] and [[Anthony Hamilton (fighter)|Anthony Hamilton]] was expected to serve as the co-main event. However, on November 10, Filipović abruptly announced his retirement, citing a shoulder injury as the main reason and also a series of recurring injuries and other effects of [[ageing]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/cro-cop-pulls-out-of-ufc-fight-night-79-co-headliner-announces-mma-retirement|title='Cro Cop' pulls out of UFC Fight Night 79 co-headliner, announces MMA retirement|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-10|access-date=2015-11-10}} Subsequently, on the following day, UFC officials confirmed that Filipović had in fact failed a drug test. The [[United States Anti-Doping Agency|U.S. Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) notified both Filipović and the UFC that he has been provisionally suspended due to a potential Anti-Doping Policy violation. Filipovic revealed he had taken [[Growth hormone|HGH (Human Growth Hormone)]] to help recover from his injuries. Promotion officials also announced that Hamilton had been removed from the card and would be rescheduled for a new bout at a future event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/officials-recently-retired-cro-cop-first-ufc-fighter-to-fail-usada-drug-test|title=Officials: Recently retired 'Cro Cop' first UFC fighter to fail USADA drug test|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-11|access-date=2015-11-11}}\n\n[[Hyun Gyu Lim]] was expected to face [[Dominique Steele]] at the event. However, Lim pulled out of the bout on November 20 and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Dong Hyun Kim (fighter)|Dong Hyun Kim]] .{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/The-Maestro-Replaces-Lim-on-Seoul-Card|title=The \"Maestro\" replaces Lim on Seoul card|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-20|access-date=2015-11-20}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (UFC Fight Pass){{cite bonuses:{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/ufc-fight-night-79-bonuses-dominique-steeles-slam-ko-is-a-50000-no-brainer|title=UFC Fight Night 79 bonuses: Dominique Steele's slam KO is a Choi''' and '''Dominique Steele'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn November 24, 2017 at the Seoul Central District Court in South Korea, [[Tae Hyun Bang]] was sentenced to a 10-month in prison, for accepting bribes in connection to throw his fight against Leo Kuntz for US$92,160, where he bet half of the bribe money on Kuntz. The three \"brokers\" who set up the plot were also sentenced. UFC officials warned both fighters of potential fight fixing when they noticed a big odd shift in the betting lines leading into the event. Therefore, Bang abandoned his plan and went on to win the fight. It was also reported that some mafia members issued death threats on Bang for they had bet US$1.7 million in favor of Kuntz to win.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/report-tae-hyun-bang-sentenced-to-10-month-prison-term-for-fight-fixing-scheme-at-ufc-fight-night-79|title=Report: Tae Hyun Bang sentenced to 10-month prison term for fight-fixing scheme at UFC Fight Night 79|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-11-24|access-date=2017-11-24}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2015 in date|2015|12|09}}\n}}\n\t\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 22''''') is an installment of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/07/conor-mcgregor-urijah-faber-coach-season-22-of-the-ultimate-fighter-u-s-vs-europe|title=Conor McGregor, Urijah Faber coach Season 22 of 'The Ultimate Fighter' – U.S. vs. Europe|date=July 12, 2015|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|access-date=July 12, 2015}}\n\nOn July 12, 2015 it was announced that the coaches for the season would be [[Conor McGregor]] and [[Urijah Faber]]. The coaches were not expected to face each other at the end of the season.\n\nThe UFC held open tryouts for the show on March 29, 2015. The casting call was for [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] fighters who are at least 21 years old and have a minimum of two wins in three professional fights.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/3/4/8150223/the-ultimate-fighter-22-tryouts-slated-for-lightweights-welterweights|title='The Ultimate Fighter 22' tryouts slated for lightweights, welterweights on March 29|date=March 4, 2015|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|access-date=March 4, 2015}} It was announced on July 12 by Dana White that 16 lightweights would be on the show – eight from the United States, and eight from Europe. In August 2015, the UFC officially announced the cast of this season, featuring 32 fighters competing in qualifying fights to enter the show.{{cite web |url=http://www.foxsports.com/watch/the-ultimate-fighter/story/ufc-ultimate-fighter-conor-mcgregor-urijah-faber-full-cast-tuf-22-announced-083115 |title=Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber full cast announced|work=foxsports.com |author=Damon Martin|date=2015-08-31|access-date=2015-08-31}}\n\n==Cast==\n\n===Teams===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n;{{color box|#848482|border=silver}} Prado\n\n{{col-end}}\n\n===Fighters===\n*'''Team McGregor (Europe):'''\n** [[Abner Lloveras]] ([[Spain]]), [[David Teymur]] ([[Sweden]]), Frantz Slioa (Sweden), Marcin Wrzosek ([[Poland]]), Martin
