Sean Carter (England). \n** '''United States''': Johnny Nuñez, Andres Quintana, Jason Soares, Brandon Ricetti, Tim Welch, Mike Flach, Austin Springer, Brennan Sevin.\n\n==Episodes==\n'''Episode 1: Europe vs. United States (September 9, 2015)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/episode-no-1-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-22-team-mcgregor-vs-team-faber |title=Episode No. 1 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 22: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber'|publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-09-10|access-date=2015-09-10}}\n* Dana White welcomed the 32 fighters, consisting of all lightweights, to the show. He introduced them to the coaches, Conor McGregor and Urijah Faber.\n* The preliminary fights began:\n:USA – '''Ryan Hall''' defeated Johnny Nuñez via submission (heel hook) in the first round.\n:Europe – '''David Teymur''' defeated Thibault Gouti via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:USA – '''Thanh Le''' defeated Andres Quintana via KO (punch) in the second round.\n:Europe – '''Mehdi Baghdad''' defeated Artem Lobov via majority decision after two rounds.\n:USA – '''Julian Erosa''' defeated Jason Soares via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:Europe – '''Saul Rogers''' defeated Paulo Boer via submission (rear-naked choke) in the second round.\n:USA – '''Billy Quarantillo''' defeated Brandon Ricetti via TKO (punches) in the second round.\n:Europe – '''Frantz Slioa''' defeated Martin Delaney via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n:USA – '''Jason Gonzaléz''' defeated Tim Welch via TKO (punches) in the second round.\n:Europe – '''Abner Lloveras''' defeated Vlado Sikic via verbal submission (shoulder injury) in the first round.\n:USA – '''Tom Gallicchio''' defeated Mike Flach via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n:Europe – '''Martin Svensson''' defeated Mohamed Gabinski via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n:Europe – '''Marcin Wrzosek''' defeated Djamil Chan via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n:Europe – '''Sascha Sharma''' defeated Sean Carter via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:USA – '''Chris Gruetzemacher''' defeated Austin Springer via TKO (strikes) in the second round.\n:USA – '''James Jenkins''' defeated Brennan Sevin via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Dana White reminds the fighters that the winning fighter and coach will get a [[Harley Davidson]] motorcycle.\n* White kicked off the tournament by flipping a coin to determine which team will pick the first fight. Team Faber wins the coin toss and will have control of fight selection on the next episode.\n\n'''Episode 2: Crowded House (September 16, 2015)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/episode-no-2-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-22-team-mcgregor-vs-team-faber|title=Episode No. 2 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 22: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber'|publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-09-17|access-date=2015-09-17}}\n* Coaches McGregor and Faber meet with their respective teams. McGregor says he doesn't feel a responsibility to coach anyone, but rather provide the team with the best mental and physical conditions possible to win fights. Faber takes a different approach than McGregor. He plans to be more hands-on and says everyone on the team must contribute in order to find successful results.\n*Dana White visits gym and reveals each coach will be granted the opportunity to bring one fighter back who was eliminated during the entry round of fights to make the “TUF 22” cast. Coach McGregor unsurprisingly selects current training partner Artem Lobov, while Faber selects Johnny Nuñez. White also announces that with two new athletes joining the show, there will be nine quarter-finalists instead of the usual eight. As a result, one winning fighter whom White determines has the worst performance in victory will not move to the next round.\n* '''Ryan Hall''' defeated Frantz Slioa via submission (heel hook) in the first round.\n* Faber announces the next fight: Chris Gruetzemacher vs. Sascha Sharma.\n\n'''Episode 3: Recognize the Enemy (September 23, 2015)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/episode-no-3-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-22-team-mcgregor-vs-team-faber|title=Episode No. 3 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 22: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber'|publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-09-23|access-date=2015-09-23}}\n* UFC President Dana White tried to explain McGregor's unique approach to coaching his team. He doesn't show up for the morning sessions, which focuses on light training. However, McGregor always arrives for the evening session, which focuses on technique.\n* The Team Faber coaches and fighters then have a night of relaxation where they watch T.J. Dillashaw's [[UFC on Fox 16]] bout against [[Renan Barão]]. Dillashaw defended his [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] at the event.\n* Prior to the official weigh-ins for the Sharma/Gruetzmacher bout, McGregor and Faber have a conversation about Dillashaw's fight. McGregor says Faber should fight Dillashaw even though they are teammates. He says Dillashaw is “the only fight for you.” Faber indicates that he is not interested in the fight, but McGregor continues to pester him over the situation.\n* '''Chris Gruetzemacher''' defeated Sascha Sharma via unanimous decision after three rounds. \n* Faber announces the next fight: Tom Gallicchio vs. Marcin Wrzosek.\n\n'''Episode 4: A Faithless Foe (September 30, 2015)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/episode-no-4-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-22-team-mcgregor-vs-team-faber|title=Episode No. 4 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 22: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber'|publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-09-30|access-date=2015-09-30}}\n* McGregor and Faber exchange words outside the dressing rooms and Faber tells McGregor that he should attend all team practices if he's going to be so angry about fight results. The coaches exchange words but McGregor remains firm in his stance that his role is not to babysit the athletes.\n* At the next Team McGregor training session, McGregor begins to change his mind on his approach to coaching. He indicates he can't help but become emotionally invested in the fights.\n* Team Faber's Johnny Nuñez, who was selected as one of the returning fighters after being defeated in the elimination round, decides to skip practice. Faber expresses frustration in the athlete at the evening training session.\n* '''Marcin Wrzosek''' defeated Tom Gallicchio via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* McGregor announces the next fight: Saul Rogers vs. Billy Quarantillo.\n\n'''Episode 5: Just Do Something (October 7, 2015)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/episode-no-5-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-22-team-mcgregor-vs-team-faber|title=Episode No. 5 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 22: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber'|publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-10-07|access-date=2015-10-07}}\n* The fighters return to the \"TUF\" house, and Team Faber's Tom Gallicchio is upset with his loss to Marcin Wrzosek on the previous episode. The 10-year veteran says he's tired of disappointments and setbacks, and he begins to question his future.\n* Coaches and fighters from both teams enjoy some relaxation time outside of the house. They travel to a Las Vegas restaurant for dinner and drinks. The fighters are grateful for the bonding time with the coaches outside of a training environment.\n* Team Faber's Chris Gruetzemacher begins to show signs of a succumbing to the pressure of the competition. Despite earning a victory in his opening-round fight, Gruetzemacher shares frustration with living in the house. He says he's not comfortable with the training tactics or the members of his team.\n* '''Saul Rogers''' defeated Billy Quarantillo via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* McGregor announces the next fight: Mehdi Baghdad vs. Julian Erosa.\n\n'''Episode 6: Snake in the Grass (October 21, 2015)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/episode-no-6-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-22-team-mcgregor-vs-team-faber|title=Episode No. 6 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 22: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber'|publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-10-21|access-date=2015-10-21}}\n* Team Faber has a guest trainer as Julian Erosa finalizes his preparation. UFC bantamweight champion and “TUF 14” runner-up [[T.J. Dillashaw]] joins the Team Faber coaching staff to help provide guidance and share knowledge with the athletes. \n* Faber explains his relationship with Dillashaw and the well-publicized riff with former head trainer [[Duane Ludwig]] and [[Team Alpha Male]]. Dana White discusses McGregor's pestering of Faber about the situation over the course of the season, and Dillashaw believes a confrontation with McGregor is bound to occur.\n* McGregor, Faber and [[Cody Garbrandt]] engage in an argument over the Dillashaw situation. McGregor states that T.J. is simply appearing on the show to better his own interests. He goes on to predict TJ's apparent departure from Team Alpha Male, some months later. When Conor continues to press the issue, a member of Faber's team attempts to speak up. Conor challenges him to \"do something\" about it, when Garbrandt engages and proceeds to push him. Conor does not budge and a skirmish erupts between both teams. The fighters eventually calm down and the teams separate. Dillashaw arrives and McGregor confronts him about his relationship with Ludwig. T.J. simply has no real response back.\n* '''Julian Erosa''' defeated Mehdi Baghdad via majority decision after two rounds.\n* Faber announces the next fight: Thanh Le vs. Martin Svensson.\n\n'''Episode 7: Mental Breakdown (November 4, 2015)'''\n\n* After a disagreement on who had the most boring fight between the two, Team Faber members Chris Gruetzemacher and Julian Erosa get into a heated argument that leads to a drunken confrontation at the \"TUF\" house. This causes the rest of their teammates and the Team McGregor members to break it up before it gets physical.\n* In order to dress up like McGregor for the fight, as a joke, Faber takes his team to the local [[thrift store]] to buy cheesy clothes at a fraction of the cost to McGregor's highly expensive fancy threads.\n* '''Martin Svensson''' defeated Thanh Le via submission (rear-naked choke) in the second round.\n* McGregor announces the next fight: James Jenkins vs. Artem Lobov.\n\n'''Episode 8: Heart Is not Enough (November 11, 2015)'''\n\n* '''Artem Lobov''' defeated James Jenkins via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n* '''David Teymur''' defeated Johnny Nuñez via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n* McGregor announces the final fight: Abner Lloveras vs. Jason Gonzaléz.\n\n'''Episode 9: Bullseye (November 18, 2015)'''\n\n*Faber and McGregor compete in this season's coaches challenge; a target game consisting of dropping watermelons off a helicopter. Faber wins the challenge much to the dismay of McGregor. \n* '''Abner Lloveras''' defeated Jason Gonzaléz via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n*Before the quarter-final match-ups are announced, Chris Gruetzemacher is the winning preliminary fighter chosen not to advance to the quarter-finals. \n*The quarter final match-ups are announced as:\n:*Artem Lobov vs. Martin Svensson\n:*Saul Rogers vs. Ryan Hall\n:*Marcin Wrzosek vs. David Teymur\n:*Abner Lloveras vs. Julian Erosa\n\n'''Episode 10: Nice and Flowy (November 25, 2015)'''\n\n*After chosen not to advance to the quarter-finals, Chris Gruetzemacher gets a second chance when Martin Svensson's elbow is sore from his last fight against Thanh Le's kimura attempt. He goes to the doctor and an MRI showed his elbow is broken and he is eliminated from the show.\n*Dana throws the fighters a pool party at the \"TUF\" house for having exciting performances this season, and to relax, recharge and have fun.\n*'''Artem Lobov''' defeated Chris Gruetzemacher via KO (punches) in the second round.\n*'''Saul Rogers''' defeated Ryan Hall via majority decision after two rounds.\n\n'''Episode 11: Bone on Bone (December 2, 2015)'''\n*'''Marcin Wrzosek''' defeated David Teymur via majority decision after two rounds.\n*'''Julian Erosa''' defeated Abner Lloveras via split decision after three rounds.\n*After the fight, Artem Lobov calls out Julian Erosa, believing Erosa was \"gifted\" two decisions and asks Dana White to make this fight happen next.\n*The semi-final match-ups are announced as:\n:*Saul Rogers vs. Marcin Wrzosek \n:*Artem Lobov vs. Julian Erosa\n\n'''Episode 12: Here For Myself (December 9, 2015)'''\n*'''Saul Rogers''' defeated Marcin Wrzosek via submission (rear-naked choke) in the second round.\n*UFC Featherweight champion [[José Aldo]] visits the TUF gym to train Team Faber, showing the fighters pointers with their Brazilian jiu-jitsu. \n*'''Artem Lobov''' defeated Julian Erosa via KO (punches) in the first round.\n*A fast forward interview with Dana White reveals that Saul Rogers lied during his visa application and had problems getting into the United States again. Therefore, he was pulled from the finale and replaced by Ryan Hall.\n*Conor McGregor squares up with José Aldo after he turned up to assist Team Faber in their training.\n\n==Tournament (Technical) Knockout\n|-\n|}\n{{Clear}}\n\n==The Ultimate Fighter 22 Finale==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber Finale\n|image=TUF 22 Finale event poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2015|12|11}}\n|venue=[[Cosmopolitan of Las Vegas|The Chelsea at The Cosmopolitan]]\n|city= [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]]\n|attendance=1,800{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/tuf-22-finale-draws-sellout-for-450000-gate-in-las-vegas|title=TUF 22 Finale draws sellout for $450,000 gate in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-12|access-date=2015-12-12}}\n|gate=$450,000\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Namajunas vs. VanZant]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 194|UFC 194: Aldo vs. McGregor]]\n}}\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 22 Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on December 11, 2015, at [[Cosmopolitan of Las Vegas|The Chelsea at The Cosmopolitan]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], Nevada.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/TUF-22-Finale-Heads-to-Vegas-on-Dec-11|title=TUF 22 Finale heads to Vegas on Dec. 11|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-08-19|access-date=2015-08-19}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/three-cards-in-three-days-in-las-vegas-ufc-fight-night-80-set-for-dec-10|title=Three cards in three days in Las Vegas: UFC Fight Night 80 set for Dec. 10|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-09-04|access-date=2015-09-04}}\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a featherweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] and three-time title challenger [[Chad Mendes]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2015/8/25/9208815/ufc-tuf-report-frankie-edgar-vs-chad-mendes-mma-news|title=Frankie Edgar vs. Chad Mendes to headline TUF 22 Finale|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Lucas Rezende|date=2015-08-25|access-date=2015-08-25}}\n\n[[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] was expected to face [[Tony Ferguson]] at the event. However, Nurmagomedov pulled out of the fight on October 30 due to a rib injury and was replaced by [[Edson Barboza]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/30/9645620/edson-barboza-steps-in-for-khabib-nurmagomedov-to-face-tony-ferguson|title=Edson Barboza steps in for Khabib Nurmagomedov to face Tony Ferguson at TUF Finale |publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-10-30|access-date=2015-10-30}}\n\n[[Mirsad Bektic]] was expected to face [[Tatsuya Kawajiri]] at the event. However, Bektic pulled out of the fight on November 27 citing injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/reports-mirsad-bektic-out-vs-tatsuya-kawajiri-at-tuf-22-finale-in-las-vegas|title=Mirsad Bektic out vs. Tatsuya Kawajiri at TUF 22 Finale in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-27|access-date=2015-11-27}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jason Knight (fighter)|Jason Knight]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/All-Eyes-on-145|title=All eyes on 145|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-12-01|access-date=2015-12-01}}\n\n[[Justin Scoggins]] was expected to face Joby Sanchez at this event. However, Scoggins was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by [[Geane Herrera]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Herrera-Returns-to-Face-Sanchez-Next-Week|title=Herrera returns to face Sanchez next week|publisher=UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2015-12-03|date=2015-12-03}}\n\nSaul Rogers was expected to face [[Artem Lobov]] in the Lightweight final of this season. However, Rogers was not allowed to participate after it was discovered that he had falsified information on his [[Visa (document)|visa]] application, restricting his travel to the United States. In turn, Rogers was replaced by fellow cast member [[Ryan Hall (grappler)|Ryan Hall]]. Subsequently, the event will be the second to feature two fighters who had previously been eliminated from the competition to reach the finals, the first being [[The Ultimate Fighter 11]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/after-visa-issue-tuf-22-concludes-with-surprising-tournament-final|title=After visa issue, 'TUF 22' concludes with surprising tournament final|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2015-12-10|date=2015-12-10}} It was later announced that the UFC decided to cut Rogers from the organization due to the incident.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/saul-rogers-cut-by-ufc-after-visa-issue-scraps-tuf-22-final|title=Saul Rogers cut by UFC after visa issue scraps 'TUF 22' final|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|access-date=2015-12-12|date=2015-12-12}}\n\nOn February 10, it was announced that Konstantin Erokhin tested positive for [[drostanolone]] in a pre-fight, in-competition test. The test was administered by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC), not a test administered by [[USADA]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/10/10959806/konstantin-erokhin-tests-positive-for-drostanolone-at-the-ultimate|title=Konstantin Erokhin tests positive for drostanolone at The Ultimate Fighter 22 Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-02-10|access-date=2016-02-10}} On March 23, the NSAC announced he was suspended for 12 months and fined $3,300 retroactive to the date fight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/russian-heavyweight-konstantin-erokhin-suspended-12-months-for-anabolic-steroid-use|title=Russian heavyweight Konstantin Erokhin suspended 12 months for anabolic steroid use|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-23|access-date=2016-03-23}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent 2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Dillashaw vs. Cruz]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 195: Lawler vs. Condit''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on January 2, 2016, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], Nevada.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Lawler-Condit-to-Headline-UFC-195|title=Lawler-Condit to headline UFC 195|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-09-03|access-date=2015-09-03}}{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/9/4/9259981/robbie-lawler-vs-carlos-condit-title-fight-rebooked-as-ufc-195-main|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Carlos Condit title fight rebooked as UFC 195 main event |publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-09-04|access-date=2015-09-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was expected to feature a [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between then-undefeated champion [[Ronda Rousey]] and [[Holly Holm]], a former 18-time [[boxing]] world champion in three weight classes.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-ronda-rousey-vs-holly-holm-headlines-ufc-195-on-jan-2-in-las-vegas-082115|title=Ronda Rousey vs. Holly Holm headlines UFC 195 on January 2 in Las Vegas |work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2015-08-21|access-date=2015-08-21}} However, the bout was moved to [[UFC 193]] after a thumb injury to [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]] forced him out of his title bout against [[Carlos Condit]], a former [[List of World Extreme Cagefighting champions#Welterweight Championship|WEC Welterweight Champion]] and former interim UFC Welterweight Champion on UFC 193.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-193-robbie-lawler-carlos-condit-thumb-injury-ronda-rousey-holly-holm-rescheduled-082915|title=Robbie Lawler suffers thumb injury, fight with Carlos Condit expected to be rescheduled|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2015-08-29|access-date=2015-08-29}} The Lawler/Condit pairing remained intact and headlined this event.\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Sheldon Westcott]] and [[Edgar García (mixed martial artist)|Edgar García]], originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Namajunas vs. VanZant]] was moved to this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/sheldon-westcott-vs-edgar-garcia-moves-to-ufc-195-in-january|title=Sheldon Westcott vs. Edgar Garcia moves to UFC 195 in January|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-10-19|access-date=2015-10-19}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Dustin Poirier]] and [[Joseph Duffy (fighter)|Joseph Duffy]] was originally booked as main event for [[UFC Fight Night: Holohan vs. Smolka]]. However, the bout was canceled on fight week due to Duffy sustaining a concussion during a sparring session.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/21/9587444/joseph-duffy-out-of-ufc-fight-night-76-paddy-holohan-vs-louis-smolka|title=Joseph Duffy out of UFC Fight Night 76, Paddy Holohan vs. Louis Smolka set as new main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2015-10-21|access-date=2015-10-28}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/scrapped-dustin-poirier-vs-joseph-duffy-fight-rebooked-for-ufc-195-in-las-vegas|title=Scrapped Dustin Poirier vs. Joseph Duffy fight rebooked for UFC 195 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-10-28|access-date=2015-10-28}}\n\n[[Neil Magny]] was briefly scheduled to face [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] at the event. However, on November 3, Magny was pulled from the pairing in favor of a matchup with [[Kelvin Gastelum]] at [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America 2#The Ultimate Fighter Latin America 2 Finale: Magny vs. Gastelum|The Ultimate Fighter Latin America 2 Finale]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/3/9667114/kelvin-gastelum-vs-neil-magny-to-headline-tuf-latin-america-finale|title=Kelvin Gastelum vs. Neil Magny to headline TUF Latin America Finale with Matt Brown out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-11-03|access-date=2015-11-03}} Thompson was expected to remain on the card, but on December 13 it was announced that he was going to face former UFC Welterweight champion [[Johny Hendricks]] at [[UFC 196]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-196-adds-hendricks-thompson-nelson-rosholt-burkman-nonon-pyle-spencer|title=UFC 196 adds Hendricks-Thompson, Nelson-Rosholt, Burkman-Noons, Pyle-Spencer|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Bohn and Matt Erickson|date=2015-12-13|access-date=2015-12-13}}\n\n[[Russell Doane]] was briefly scheduled to face [[Michinori Tanaka]] at the event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/russell-doane-vs-michinori-tanaka-added-to-ufc-195|title=Russell Doane vs. Michinori Tanaka added to UFC 195|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-25|access-date=2015-11-30}} However, Doane was pulled from the pairing for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by former [[List of Bellator champions#Featherweight Championship|Bellator Featherweight champion]] and one time [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Joe Soto]].{{cite news|url=http://www.fightline.com/joe-soto-steps-fight-ufc-195/|title=Joe Soto steps in to fight at UFC 195|publisher=fightline.com|author=Dana Becker|date=2015-11-30|access-date=2015-11-30}}\n\n[[Erik Koch]] was expected to face [[Drew Dober]] at this event. However, on December 4, Koch was forced to pull out of the event due to injury and was replaced by [[Scott Holtzman]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Koch-Out-Holtzman-in-at-UFC-195|title=Koch out Hotlzman in at UFC 195|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2015-12-04|date=2015-12-04}}\n\n[[Kelvin Gastelum]] was expected to face [[Kyle Noke]] at this event. However, on December 22, Gastelum was forced to pull out due to a wrist injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Alex Morono]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/22/10655970/injured-kelvin-gastelum-out-of-ufc-195|title=Injured Kelvin Gastelum out of UFC 195|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Danny Segura|access-date=2015-12-22|date=2015-12-22}}\n\nOn March 23, it was announced that [[Diego Brandão]] tested positive for [[marijuana]] metabolites and was issued a temporary suspension by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC).{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/23/11292322/diego-brandao-issued-temporary-suspension-for-ufc-195-failed-drug-test|title=Diego Brandão issued temporary suspension for UFC 195 failed drug test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|access-date=2016-03-23|date=2016-03-23}} He was eventually suspended on May 19 for a period of nine months by [[USADA]], retroactive to the date of the fight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/usada-hands-diego-brandao-nine-month-suspension-following-ufc-release|title=USADA hands Diego Brandao 9-month suspension following UFC release|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-05-19|access-date=2016-05-20}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main known as '''''UFC Fight Night 80''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on December 10, 2015, at [[Cosmopolitan of Las Vegas|The Chelsea at The Cosmopolitan]] in [[Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/three-cards-in-three-days-in-las-vegas-ufc-fight-night-80-set-for-dec-10|title=Three cards in three days in Las Vegas: UFC Fight Night 80 set for and [[Joanne Wood|Joanne Calderwood]].{{Cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/VanZant-vs-Calderwood-Headlines-on-UFC-FIGHT-PASS|title=VanZant vs. Calderwood on UFC Fight Pass|publisher=UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi\"|access-date=2015-09-17|date=2015-09-17}} However on October 28, Calderwood was forced to pull out of the event and was replaced by [[Rose Namajunas]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/rose-namajunas-replaces-joanne-calderwood-meets-paige-vanzant-at-ufc-fight-night-80|title=Rose Namajunas replaces Joanne Calderwood, meets Paige VanZant at UFC Fight Night 80|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2015-10-28|date=2015-10-28}} \n\nThe event was the first that the promotion has hosted at the venue and the first of three events in as many days in the [[Las Vegas Valley]]. It also marked the first North American card to air live exclusively on their subscription-based digital network, [[UFC Fight Pass]].\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Sheldon Westcott]] and [[Edgar García (mixed martial artist)|Edgar García]], originally scheduled for this event was moved to [[UFC 195]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/sheldon-westcott-vs-edgar-garcia-moves-to-ufc-195-in-january|title=Sheldon Westcott vs. Edgar Garcia moves to UFC 195 in January|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-10-19|access-date=2015-10-19}}\n\n[[Lyman Good]] was expected to face [[Omari Akhmedov]] at the event. However, Good was pulled from the bout in late October and was replaced by [[Sérgio Moraes]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/28/9633104/lyman-good-out-sergio-moraes-in-against-omari-akhmedov-at-ufc-fight|title=Lyman Good out, Sergio Moraes in against Omari Akhmedov at UFC Fight Night 80|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=MMAFighting Newswire|date=2015-10-28|access-date=2015-10-28}}\n\n[[Michael Graves (fighter)|Michael Graves]] was expected to face [[Danny Roberts (fighter)|Danny Roberts]] at the event. However, Graves pulled out of the bout on November 30 citing an injury and was replaced by [[Nathan Coy]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/nathan-coy-in-against-danny-roberts-at-ufc-fight-night-80|title=Nathan Coy in against Danny Roberts at UFC Fight Night 80|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-11-30|access-date=2015-11-30}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (UFC Fight Pass)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Rose Namajunas]]\n|def.\n|[[Paige VanZant]]\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)|5|2:25\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Michael Chiesa]]\n|def.\n|[[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]]\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|2\n|2:57\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Sage Northcutt]]\n|def.\n|[[Cody Pfister]]\n|Submission (guillotine choke)\n|2\n|0:41\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Thiago Santos]]\n|def.\n|[[Elias Theodorou]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–27, 29–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (UFC Fight Pass)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Tim Means]]\n|def.\n|[[John Howard (fighter)|John Howard]]\n|KO (punch)\n|2\n|0:21\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Sérgio Moraes]]\n|def.\n|[[Omari Akhmedov]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|3\n|2:18\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Antônio Carlos Júnior]]\n|vs.\n|[[Kevin Casey (fighter)|Kevin Casey]]\n|No Contest (accidental eye poke)\n|1\n|0:11\n|An accidental eye poke rendered Casey unable to continue; Dillashaw]] and former [[WEC Bantamweight Championship|WEC]] and UFC Bantamweight champion [[Dominick Cruz]] headlined this event.\n\n[[Beneil Dariush]] was expected to face [[Mairbek Taisumov]] at this event. However, on December 3, Dariush was forced to pull out of the bout due to injury. He was replaced by [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]].{{cite web|url=https://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/islip-s-chris-wade-books-fight-vs-mairbek-taisumov-at-ufc-fight-night-81-in-boston-u42188|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20151209181013/http://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/islip-s-chris-wade-books-fight-vs-mairbek-taisumov-at-ufc-fight-night-81-in-boston-1.11190004|url-status=live|archive-date=December 9, 2015|title=Islip's Chris Wade books fight vs. Mairbek Taisumov at UFC Fight Night 81 in Boston|publisher=newsday.com|author=Mark La Monica|access-date=2015-12-03|date=2015-12-03}} In turn, Taisumov was pulled from the fight during the week leading to the event due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Mehdi Baghdad]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/visa-issues-force-taisumov-off-ufc-fight-night-81-medhi-baghdad-in-vs-chris-wade|title=Visa issues force Taisumov off UFC Fight Night 81, Medhi Baghdad in vs. Chris Wade|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-11|access-date=2016-01-11}}\n\n[[Dennis Bermudez]] was expected to face [[Maximo Blanco]] at this event. However, on December 5, Bermudez pulled out of the bout due to a [[staph infection]] in his shin.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/5/9852982/dennis-bermudez-suffers-staph-infection-out-of-january-bout-against-Maximo-Blanco|title=Dennis Bermudez suffers staph infection, out of January UFC Fight Night 81 bout|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Hunter A. Homistek|access-date=2015-12-05|date=2015-12-06}} On January 4, it was announced that he was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Luke Sanders]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/luke-sanders-steps-in-to-fight-maximo-blanco-ufc-fight-night-81-lineup-shuffled|title=Luke Sanders steps in to fight Maximo Blanco; UFC Fight Night 81 lineup shuffled|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-01-04|date=2016-01-04|archive-date=2016-01-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160106140143/http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/luke-sanders-steps-in-to-fight-maximo-blanco-ufc-fight-night-81-lineup-shuffled|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Patrick Williams (fighter)|Patrick Williams]] was expected to face [[Rob Font]] at this event. However, Williams was forced to pull out due to injury in late December and was replaced by promotional newcomer Joey Gomez.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/newcomer-joey-gomez-gets-fill-in-duties-at-ufc-fight-night-81-in-boston|title=Newcomer Joey Gomez gets fill-in duties at UFC Fight Night 81 in Boston|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2015-12-31|date=2015-12-31}}\n\n[[Jimy Hettes]] was expected to face [[Charles Rosa]]. However, on January 10, only 8 days before the event, Hettes pulled out due to injury.{{cite web|url=http://themmacorner.com/2016/01/10/jimy-hettes-out-of-ufc-fight-night-81-bout-against-charles-rosa-in-boston/|title=Jimy Hettes out of UFC Fight Night 81 bout against Charles Rosa in Boston|publisher=themmacorner.com|author=José Youngs|access-date=2016-01-10|date=2016-01-10}} He was very briefly replaced by promotional newcomer [[Augusto Mendes]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/augusto-mendes-elvis-mutapcic-make-debuts-on-short-notice-at-ufc-fight-night-81|title=Augusto Mendes, Elvis Mutapcic make debuts on short notice at UFC Fight Night 81|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-01-11|date=2016-01-11}} Just two days later, Mendes was removed from the fight and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Kyle Bochniak]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/newcomer-kyle-bochniak-a-late-addition-to-sundays-ufc-fight-night-81-lineup|title=Newcomer Kyle Bochniak a late addition to Sunday's UFC Fight Night 81 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-01-14|date=2016-01-14}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Abdul-Kerim Edilov]] was expected to face [[Francimar Barroso]] at the event. However, Edilov pulled out of the fight in the days leading up to the event citing a knee injury and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Elvis Mutapčić]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/1/10/10744476/ufc-fight-night-dillashaw-vs-cruz-injury-abdul-kerim-edilov-torn-meniscus-russian-prospect-mma|title=Torn meniscus forces Russian prospect off UFC Fight Night: Dillashaw vs. Cruz|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Karim Zidan|access-date=2016-01-11|date=2016-01-11|archive-date=2016-01-12|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160112211636/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/1/10/10744476/ufc-fight-night-dillashaw-vs-cruz-injury-abdul-kerim-edilov-torn-meniscus-russian-prospect-mma|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Dominick Cruz]] \n|def.\n|[[T.J. in [[Newark, New Jersey]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Returns-to-Newark-for-FOX-Show-in-Jan|title=UFC returns to Newark for FOX show in Jan.|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2015-11-04|access-date=2015-11-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a potential [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] title eliminator bout between [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Nogueira vs. Team Mir]] winner [[Ryan Bader]].\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Welterweight Championship|Strikeforce Welterweight Champion]] [[Tarec Saffiedine]] and [[Jake Ellenberger]] was originally booked as the main event of [[UFC Fight Night: Saffiedine vs. Lim]] on January 4, 2014, and later as part of [[UFC 172]] on April 26, 2014. However, the bout was cancelled both times due to Ellenberger having a hamstring injury {{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/11/30/5160544/jake-ellenberger-reveals-hamstring-injury-forced-him-out-of-ufc-fight|title=Jake Ellenberger reveals hamstring injury forced him out of UFC Fight Night 34|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2013-11-30|access-date=2016-01-29}} and Saffiedine pulling out due to an undisclosed injury, respectively.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/with-saffiedine-injured-ellenberger-vs-lawler-booked-for-ufc-173|title= With Saffiedine injured, Ellenberger vs. Lawler booked for UFC 173|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2014-03-28|access-date=2016-01-29}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/4/9672618/jake-ellenberger-vs-tarec-saffiedine-three-other-fights-added-to-ufc|title=Jake Ellenberger vs. Tarec Saffiedine added to UFC on FOX 18|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-11-04|access-date=2016-01-29}}\n\n[[Joaquim Silva (fighter)|Joaquim Silva]] was expected to face [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] at this event. However, on December 23, Silva was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by [[Carlos Diego Ferreira]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-on-fox-18-gets-two-new-bouts-one-injury-replacement|title=UFC on FOX 18 gets two new bouts, one injury replacement|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2015-12-23|date=2015-12-23}}\n\n[[Andrew Holbrook]] was expected to face [[Sage Northcutt]] in a lightweight bout at this event. However, on January 22, Holbrook was pulled out of the fight due to injury and was replaced by [[Bryan Barberena]]. The bout took place at welterweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/andrew-holbrook-out-bryan-barberena-meets-sage-northcutt-at-ufc-on-fox-18|title=Andrew Holbrook out, Bryan Barberena meets Sage Northcutt at UFC on FOX 18|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-01-22|date=2016-01-22}}\n\nOn March 11, it was announced that Felipe Olivieri was notified of a potential anti-doping policy violation due to a positive test for [[methyltestosterone]] stemming from an out-of-competition sample in Brazil, before the event. Oliveiri stated he never used any illegal substance, and will ask for the B-sample to be tested.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/11/11204046/felipe-olivieri-flagged-for-potential-usada-anti-doping-violation|title=Felipe Olivieri flagged for potential USADA anti-doping million|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=February 7, 2016}}\n|gate=$1,435,000\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Bader]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Cowboy]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Hendricks vs. Thompson''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 82''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on February 6, 2016, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], Nevada.{{cite news|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Dana-White-Confirms-WerdumVelasquez-Rematch-for-UFC-196-in-Las-Vegas-96921|title=Dana White confirms Werdum-Velasquez rematch for UFC 196 in Las Vegas|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2015-12-09|access-date=2015-12-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally billed as ''UFC 196'' and was scheduled to be headlined by a [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between then-current champion [[Fabrício Werdum]] and former two-time champion [[Cain Velasquez]]. A series of events eventually led to a main event shuffle: on January 24, 13 days before the event, Velasquez pulled out of the bout due to a back injury and was replaced by [[Stipe Miocic]].{{cite news|url=http://fansided.com/2016/01/24/cain-velasquez-out-fabricio-werdum-stipe-miocic-ufc-196/|title=Cain Velasquez out, Stipe Miocic to fill in at UFC 196|publisher=fansided.com|author=Mike Dyce|date=2016-01-24|access-date=2016-01-24}} On the following day, Werdum announced that he was pulling out of the event due to injury as well as he was not comfortable with the opponent change while dealing with injuries of his own.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/1/25/10828922/fabricio-werdum-pulls-out-of-ufc-196-with-injury|title=Fabricio Werdum pulls out of UFC 196 with injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-01-25|access-date=2016-01-25}}\n\nWith no viable main event options remaining, on January 26 UFC President [[Dana White]] announced the company was canceling the pay-per-view broadcast. Therefore, UFC 196 became a ''Fight Night'' card. The event aired on [[Fox Sports 1]] and was headlined by a five-round welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Johny Hendricks]] and five-time [[kickboxing]] world champion [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]]. The two were originally scheduled to meet in the co-main event.{{cite news|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/mma-cagewriter/with-heavyweight-title-fight-off--ufc-196-is-shifted-to-fox-sports-1-212916963.html|title=With heavyweight title fight off, UFC 196 is shifted to Fox Sports 1|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2016-01-26|access-date=2016-01-26}}{{cite news|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/Hendricks-Thompson-headlines-UFC-Fight-Night|title=Hendricks-Thompson headlines UFC Fight Night|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-27|access-date=2016-01-27}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[List of WEC champions#Welterweight Championship|WEC Welterweight Champion]] [[Mike Pyle (fighter)|Mike Pyle]] and [[Sean Spencer (fighter)|Sean Spencer]] was originally booked for [[UFC 187]]. However, Spencer pulled out due to injury.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/04/sean-spencer-out-at-ufc-187-promotion-seeking-opponent-for-mike-pyle|title=Sean Spencer out at UFC 187, promotion seeking opponent for Mike Pyle|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-04-23|access-date=2016-02-07}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-196-adds-hendricks-thompson-nelson-rosholt-burkman-nonon-pyle-spencer|title=UFC 196 adds Hendricks-Thompson, Nelson-Rosholt, Burkman-Noons, Pyle-Spencer|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn and Matt Erickson|date=2015-12-13|access-date=2016-02-07}}\n\n[[Mickey Gall]], whose first pro bout and subsequent call-out of former [[WWE Championship|WWE Champion]] [[CM Punk]] were featured on [[UFC Fight Pass]]' ''Lookin' for a Fight'' reality show, faced fellow newcomer [[Mike Jackson (fighter)|Mike Jackson]]. With his win, Gall was able to secure the matchup with Punk, which eventually took place in September 2016 at [[UFC 203]].{{cite news|url=https://www.espn.com/blog/mma/post/_/id/22893/the-cm-punk-effect-hits-ufc-with-mickey-gall-michael-jackson|title=The CM Punk effect hits UFC with Mickey Gall-Michael Jackson bout|publisher=[[ESPN]]|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2016-02-03}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]]\n|def.\n|[[Johny Hendricks]] |TKO (punches)\n|1\n|3:31\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Roy Nelson (fighter)|Roy Nelson]]\n|def.\n|[[Jared Rosholt]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Ovince Saint Preux]]\n|def.\n|[[Rafael Cavalcante]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Flyweight\n|[[Joseph Benavidez]]\n|def.\n|[[Zach Makovsky]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Misha Cirkunov]]\n|def.\n|[[Alex Nicholson (fighter)|Alex Nicholson]]\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|2\n|1:28\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Mike Pyle (fighter)|Mike Pyle]]\n|def.\n|[[Sean Spencer (fighter)|Sean Spencer]]\n|TKO (elbows and knees)\n|3\n|4:25\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Josh Burkman]]\n|def.\n|[[K.
